RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.existsUnique_capParameter (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  ∃! q, 1 ≤ q ∧ Real.log q + 2 / 3 * (q ^ 3 - 1) = Real.log C
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter_spec (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  1 ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C ∧
    Real.log (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C) +
        2 / 3 * (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C ^ 3 - 1) =
      Real.log C
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter_eq_one_iff (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C = 1 ↔ C = 1
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.orderedTriangle : Set (ℝ × ℝ) :=
{p | 0 ≤ p.1 ∧ p.1 < p.2 ∧ p.2 ≤ 1}
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ :=
fun k ↦ ∫ (p : ℝ × ℝ) in RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.orderedTriangle, 2 * p.2 * Real.exp (k p.1 - k p.2)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairMarginal : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun g v ↦
  (∫ (s : ℝ), RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairWeight g (v, s)) -
    ∫ (t : ℝ), RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairWeight g (t, v)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairRemainder : (ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ :=
fun k g ↦
  ∫ (p : ℝ × ℝ) in RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.orderedTriangle,
    2 * p.2 * Real.exp (g p.1 - g p.2) *
      (Real.exp (k p.1 - g p.1 - (k p.2 - g p.2)) - 1 - (k p.1 - g p.1 - (k p.2 - g p.2)))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_sub_eq_integral_marginal (k g : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hg : ContinuousOn g (Set.Icc 0 1)) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional k - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional g =
    (∫ (v : ℝ), RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairMarginal g v * (k v - g v)) +
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairRemainder k g
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairRemainder_nonneg (k g : ℝ → ℝ) :
  0 ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairRemainder k g
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_le_of_firstVariation_nonneg (k g : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hg : ContinuousOn g (Set.Icc 0 1))
  (hfirst : 0 ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFirstVariation k g) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional g ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional k
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_congr (k g : ℝ → ℝ) (h : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, k v = g v) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional k = RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional g
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.strictMonoOn_capParameter :
  StrictMonoOn RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter (Set.Ici 1)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact : ℝ → ℝ :=
fun q ↦ 1 / √(q ^ 4 + 2 * q)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact : ℝ → ℝ :=
fun q ↦ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q * q ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.contact_bounds (q : ℝ) (hq : 1 ≤ q) :
  0 < RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q ∧
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact q ∧
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact q < 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.one_sub_upperContact_sq (q : ℝ) (hq : 0 < q) :
  1 - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact q ^ 2 =
    2 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q ^ 2 * q
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.freeExponential : ℝ → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun a v ↦ √(v / a) * Real.exp (2 / 3 * (√(v / a) ^ 3 - 1))
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.freeLeftPrimitive : ℝ → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun a v ↦ a * Real.exp (2 / 3 * (√(v / a) ^ 3 - 1))
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.freeRightPrimitive : ℝ → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun a v ↦ -2 * a ^ 2 * Real.exp (-(2 / 3 * (√(v / a) ^ 3 - 1)))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.integral_freeExponential (a c d : ℝ) (ha : 0 < a) (hc : 0 < c) (hd : 0 < d) :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in c..d, RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.freeExponential a x =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.freeLeftPrimitive a d - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.freeLeftPrimitive a c
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.integral_two_mul_div_freeExponential (a c d : ℝ) (ha : 0 < a) (hc : 0 < c)
  (hd : 0 < d) :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in c..d, 2 * x / RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.freeExponential a x =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.freeRightPrimitive a d -
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.freeRightPrimitive a c
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleExponential : ℝ → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun q v ↦
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.freeExponential (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q)
    (max (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q)
      (min v (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact q)))
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile : ℝ → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun C v ↦
  Real.log
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleExponential (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C) v)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleExponential_eq_piecewise (q v : ℝ) (hq : 1 ≤ q) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleExponential q v =
    if v ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q then 1
    else
      if v ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact q then
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.freeExponential (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q) v
      else Real.exp (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.logCap q)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile_admissible (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  ContinuousOn (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C) (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
    ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
      0 ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C v ∧
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C v ≤ Real.log C
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile_one (v : ℝ) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile 1 v = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.contact_separation_iff (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C) <
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C) ↔
    1 < C
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exp_tangent_quadratic_lower (m x y : ℝ) (hx : m ≤ x) (hy : m ≤ y) :
  Real.exp m / 2 * (x - y) ^ 2 ≤ Real.exp x - Real.exp y - Real.exp y * (x - y)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstaclePrefix : ℝ → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun q v ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..v, RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleExponential q x
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleTail : ℝ → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun q v ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ) in v..1, 2 * x / RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleExponential q x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstaclePrefix_eq_low (q v : ℝ) (hq : 1 ≤ q)
  (hv : v ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstaclePrefix q v = v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstaclePrefix_eq_free (q v : ℝ) (hq : 1 ≤ q)
  (hl : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q ≤ v)
  (hu : v ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact q) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstaclePrefix q v =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.freeLeftPrimitive (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q) v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstaclePrefix_eq_high (q v : ℝ) (hq : 1 ≤ q)
  (hv : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact q ≤ v) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstaclePrefix q v =
    Real.exp (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.logCap q) *
      (v - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact q + RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q / q)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleTail_eq_low (q v : ℝ) (hq : 1 ≤ q)
  (hv : v ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleTail q v =
    3 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q ^ 2 - v ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleTail_eq_free (q v : ℝ) (hq : 1 ≤ q)
  (hl : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q ≤ v)
  (hu : v ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact q) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleTail q v =
    -RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.freeRightPrimitive (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact q) v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleTail_eq_high (q v : ℝ) (hq : 1 ≤ q)
  (hv : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact q ≤ v) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleTail q v =
    (1 - v ^ 2) / Real.exp (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.logCap q)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairMarginal_eq_intervalIntegrals (k : ℝ → ℝ) (v : ℝ) (hv : v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairMarginal k v =
    (Real.exp (k v) * ∫ (s : ℝ) in v..1, 2 * s / Real.exp (k s)) -
      2 * v / Real.exp (k v) * ∫ (t : ℝ) in 0..v, Real.exp (k t)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairMarginal_eq_zero_of_not_mem (k : ℝ → ℝ) (v : ℝ) (hv : v ∉ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairMarginal k v = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.integrable_pairMarginal (k : ℝ → ℝ) (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairMarginal k) MeasureTheory.volume
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_eq_intervalIntegral (k : ℝ → ℝ) (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional k =
    ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, 2 * s / Real.exp (k s) * ∫ (v : ℝ) in 0..s, Real.exp (k v)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairMarginal_obstacleLogProfile_low (C v : ℝ) (hv : 0 ≤ v)
  (hl : v ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C)) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairMarginal (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C) v =
    3 *
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C) ^ 2 - v ^ 2)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairMarginal_obstacleLogProfile_free (C v : ℝ)
  (hl : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C) ≤ v)
  (hu : v ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C)) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairMarginal (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C) v = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairMarginal_obstacleLogProfile_high (C v : ℝ)
  (hu : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C) ≤ v)
  (hv : v ≤ 1) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairMarginal (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C) v =
    -3 * (v - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C)) *
      (v +
        1 / (3 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C)))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_obstacleLogProfile_le (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (k : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ k v ∧ k v ≤ Real.log C) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C) ≤
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional k
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_obstacleLogProfile_eq (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C) =
    2 / 3 - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C) +
      1 / (3 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_obstacleLogProfile_one :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile 1) = 2 / 3
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairRemainder_eq_zero_iff (k g : ℝ → ℝ) (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1))
  (hg : ContinuousOn g (Set.Icc 0 1)) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairRemainder k g = 0 ↔ ∃ c, ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, k v - g v = c
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.hasDerivWithinAt_obstacleLogProfile_lowerContact_Iic (C : ℝ) :
  HasDerivWithinAt (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C) 0
    (Set.Iic (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C)))
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.hasDerivWithinAt_obstacleLogProfile_lowerContact_Ici (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 < C) :
  HasDerivWithinAt (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C)
    (3 / (2 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C)))
    (Set.Ici (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C)))
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.hasDerivWithinAt_obstacleLogProfile_upperContact_Iic (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 < C) :
  HasDerivWithinAt (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C)
    (1 / (2 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C)) +
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C /
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C))
    (Set.Iic (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C)))
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.hasDerivWithinAt_obstacleLogProfile_upperContact_Ici (C : ℝ) :
  HasDerivWithinAt (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C) 0
    (Set.Ici (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C)))
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.not_differentiableAt_obstacleLogProfile_lowerContact (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 < C) :
  ¬DifferentiableAt ℝ (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C)
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.not_differentiableAt_obstacleLogProfile_upperContact (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 < C) :
  ¬DifferentiableAt ℝ (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C)
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_eq_obstacleLogProfile_iff (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (k : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ k v ∧ k v ≤ Real.log C) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional k =
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C) ↔
    Set.EqOn k (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C) (Set.Icc 0 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_ge_closedForm (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (k : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ k v ∧ k v ≤ Real.log C) :
  2 / 3 - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C) +
      1 / (3 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C)) ≤
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional k
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_eq_closedForm_iff (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (k : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ k v ∧ k v ≤ Real.log C) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional k =
      2 / 3 - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C) +
        1 / (3 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C)) ↔
    Set.EqOn k (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C) (Set.Icc 0 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_obstacleLogProfile_lt_of_differentiableOn (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 < C)
  (k : ℝ → ℝ) (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hd : DifferentiableOn ℝ k (Set.Ioo 0 1))
  (hbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ k v ∧ k v ≤ Real.log C) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C) <
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional k
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerObstacleWeight : ℝ → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun a v ↦ 3 * max (a ^ 2 - v ^ 2) 0 / (2 * a ^ 3)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.integral_lowerObstacleWeight (a : ℝ) (ha : 0 < a) (ha1 : a ≤ 1) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in 0..1, RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerObstacleWeight a v = 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.integral_lowerObstacleWeight_sq (a : ℝ) (ha : 0 < a) (ha1 : a ≤ 1) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in 0..1, RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerObstacleWeight a v ^ 2 = 6 / (5 * a)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFirstVariation_eq_intervalIntegral_marginal (k g : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hg : ContinuousOn g (Set.Icc 0 1)) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFirstVariation k g =
    ∫ (v : ℝ) in 0..1, RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairMarginal g v * (k v - g v)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFirstVariation_obstacleLogProfile_nonneg (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (k : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ k v ∧ k v ≤ Real.log C) :
  0 ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFirstVariation k (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.integral_lowerObstacleWeight_error_sq_le (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (k : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ k v ∧ k v ≤ Real.log C) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in 0..1,
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerObstacleWeight
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C)) v *
        (k v - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C v) ^ 2 ≤
    Real.log C /
        (2 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C) ^ 3) *
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFirstVariation k (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairRemainder_ge_quadratic_integral (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (k g : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hg : ContinuousOn g (Set.Icc 0 1))
  (hkbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ k v ∧ k v ≤ Real.log C) (hgbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ g v ∧ g v ≤ Real.log C) :
  1 / (2 * C) *
      ∫ (p : ℝ × ℝ) in RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.orderedTriangle,
        2 * p.2 * (k p.1 - g p.1 - (k p.2 - g p.2)) ^ 2 ≤
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairRemainder k g
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairQuadratic : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ :=
fun f ↦ ∫ (p : ℝ × ℝ) in RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.orderedTriangle, 2 * p.2 * (f p.1 - f p.2) ^ 2
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.unitMean : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ :=
fun f ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, f x
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.unitCentered : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun f x ↦ f x - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.unitMean f
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.centeredPrimitive : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun f x ↦ ∫ (v : ℝ) in 0..x, RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.unitCentered f v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairQuadratic_eq_centered_integrals (f : ℝ → ℝ) (hf : ContinuousOn f (Set.Icc 0 1)) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairQuadratic f =
    (∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (1 + x ^ 2) * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.unitCentered f x ^ 2) +
      2 * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.centeredPrimitive f x ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.integral_unitCentered_sq_le_pairQuadratic (f : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hf : ContinuousOn f (Set.Icc 0 1)) :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.unitCentered f x ^ 2 ≤
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairQuadratic f
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integral_sq_le_of_variance_anchor {f w : ℝ → ℝ} {P a b D : ℝ}
  (hf : ContinuousOn f (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hw : ContinuousOn w (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hw_nonneg : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ w x)
  (hw_integral : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, w x = 1) (hP : 0 ≤ P) (ha : 0 < a) (hb : 0 ≤ b)
  (hbudget : P + a * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, (f x - ∫ (v : ℝ) in 0..1, f v) ^ 2 ≤ D)
  (hanchor : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, w x * f x ^ 2 ≤ b * P) :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, f x ^ 2 ≤ ((∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, w x ^ 2) / a + b) * D
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integral_evenExtension_error_sq_eq (h e : ℝ → ℝ) (hh : ContinuousOn h (Set.Icc (-1) 1))
  (he : ContinuousOn e (Set.Icc 0 1)) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, (h v - e |v|) ^ 2 = (∫ (v : ℝ) in 0..1, (h v - e v) ^ 2) + ∫ (v : ℝ) in 0..1, (h (-v) - e v) ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integral_reflection_error_sq_le (h e : ℝ → ℝ) (hh : ContinuousOn h (Set.Icc (-1) 1))
  (he : ContinuousOn e (Set.Icc 0 1)) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, (h v - h (-v)) ^ 2 ≤
    4 * ((∫ (v : ℝ) in 0..1, (h v - e v) ^ 2) + ∫ (v : ℝ) in 0..1, (h (-v) - e v) ^ 2)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integral_symmetrization_error_sq_eq (h : ℝ → ℝ) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, (h v - (h v + h (-v)) / 2) ^ 2 = 1 / 4 * ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, (h v - h (-v)) ^ 2
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.stabilityConstant : ℝ → ℝ :=
fun C ↦
  (12 * C / (5 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C)) +
      Real.log C /
        (2 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.lowerContact (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter C) ^ 3))⁻¹
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.stabilityConstant_pos (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  0 < RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.stabilityConstant C
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_deficit_controls_L2 (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (k : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ k v ∧ k v ≤ Real.log C) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.stabilityConstant C *
      ∫ (v : ℝ) in 0..1, (k v - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C v) ^ 2 ≤
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional k -
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.hemisphereDeficit : ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ :=
fun C h ↦
  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional h +
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional fun v ↦ h (-v)) -
    2 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.hemisphereDeficit_controls_evenExtension (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hh : ContinuousOn h (Set.Icc (-1) 1)) (hbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 ≤ h v ∧ h v ≤ Real.log C) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, (h v - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.obstacleLogProfile C |v|) ^ 2 ≤
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.hemisphereDeficit C h / RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.stabilityConstant C
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.hemisphereDeficit_controls_reflection (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hh : ContinuousOn h (Set.Icc (-1) 1)) (hbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 ≤ h v ∧ h v ≤ Real.log C) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, (h v - h (-v)) ^ 2 ≤
    4 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.hemisphereDeficit C h /
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.stabilityConstant C
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.hemisphereDeficit_controls_symmetrization (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) (h : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hh : ContinuousOn h (Set.Icc (-1) 1)) (hbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 ≤ h v ∧ h v ≤ Real.log C) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, (h v - (h v + h (-v)) / 2) ^ 2 ≤
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.hemisphereDeficit C h / RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.stabilityConstant C
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_contDiff_one_sub_sq_factor_of_roots (p : ℝ → ℝ) (hp : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) p)
  (hneg : p (-1) = 0) (hpos : p 1 = 0) : ∃ b, ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) b ∧ ∀ (x : ℝ), p x = (1 - x ^ 2) * b x
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.balance : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ :=
fun a ↦ ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, v * a v
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun a v ↦ 2 * ∫ (ξ : ℝ) in v..1, ξ * a ξ
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.momentCoordinate : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun a v ↦ ∫ (ξ : ℝ) in 0..v, a ξ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.momentCoordinate_hasDerivAt (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a) (v : ℝ) :
  HasDerivAt (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.momentCoordinate a) (a v) v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp_hasDerivAt (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a) (v : ℝ) :
  HasDerivAt (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp a) (-2 * v * a v) v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.momentCoordinate_strictMonoOn (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (haPos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) :
  StrictMonoOn (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.momentCoordinate a) (Set.Icc (-1) 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp_northPole_slope (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (haPos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) :
  deriv (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp a) 1 /
      deriv (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.momentCoordinate a) 1 =
    -2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp_southPole_slope (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (haPos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) :
  deriv (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp a) (-1) /
      deriv (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.momentCoordinate a) (-1) =
    2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp_pos_of_balance (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (haPos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) (hBalance : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.balance a = 0)
  (v : ℝ) (hv : v ∈ Set.Ioo (-1) 1) : 0 < RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp a v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp_contDiff (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) a) :
  ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp a)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp_const (c v : ℝ) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp (fun x ↦ c) v = c * (1 - v ^ 2)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.balance_const (c : ℝ) :
  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.balance fun x ↦ c) = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.exists_smooth_positive_warp_factor (a : ℝ → ℝ)
  (ha : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) a) (haPos : ∀ z ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a z)
  (hBalance : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.balance a = 0) :
  ∃ b,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) b ∧
      (∀ (z : ℝ), RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp a z = (1 - z ^ 2) * b z) ∧
        b (-1) = a (-1) ∧ b 1 = a 1 ∧ ∀ z ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < b z
structure RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData : Type
number of parameters: 0
fields:
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.a : ℝ → ℝ

  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.b_contDiff : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) self.b
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.c_contDiff : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) self.c
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.a_pos : ∀ z ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < self.a z
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.b_pos : ∀ z ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < self.b z

        self.a =
      0
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.warp_factor : ∀ (z : ℝ),
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp self.a z = (1 - z ^ 2) * self.b z

      self.a z ^ 2 - self.b z ^ 2 = (1 - z ^ 2) * self.c z

    (b_pos : ∀ z ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < b z)
    (balance_eq : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.balance a = 0)
    (warp_factor : ∀ (z : ℝ), RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp a z = (1 - z ^ 2) * b z)
    (radial_factor : ∀ (z : ℝ), a z ^ 2 - b z ^ 2 = (1 - z ^ 2) * c z) :
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.exists_poleData (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) a)
  (haPos : ∀ z ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a z) (hBalance : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.balance a = 0) :
  ∃ D, D.a = a ∧ D.b (-1) = a (-1) ∧ D.b 1 = a 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.constant (r : ℝ) (hr : 0 < r) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData
@[reducible] def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere : Set (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 3)) :=
Metric.sphere 0 1
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere → ℝ :=
⇑(DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.innerCoordFun (EuclideanSpace.single 2 1))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight_contMDiff :
  ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤)
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight_mem_Icc (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection :=
DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.duSec RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight_contMDiff
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm_apply (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (v : TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm x) fun x ↦ v) = (d% RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) v
structure RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients : Type
number of parameters: 0
fields:
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.b : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere → ℝ
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.q : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere → ℝ
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.b_contMDiff : ContMDiff
      (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤) self.b
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.q_contMDiff : ContMDiff
      (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤) self.q
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.b_pos : ∀
      (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere), 0 < self.b x
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.radial_pos : ∀
      (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
      0 < self.b x + self.q x * (1 - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x ^ 2)
constructor:
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.mk
    (b q : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere → ℝ)
    (b_contMDiff : ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤) b)
    (q_contMDiff : ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤) q)
    (b_pos : ∀ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere), 0 < b x)
    (radial_pos :
      ∀ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
        0 < b x + q x * (1 - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x ^ 2)) :
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.heightOneForm_sq_le
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (v : TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm x) fun x ↦ v) ^ 2 ≤
    (1 - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x ^ 2) *
      ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round.inner x) v) v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.metric_inner_apply
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients) (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (v w : TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  ((D.metric.inner x) v) w =
    D.b x * ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round.inner x) v) w +
      D.q x *
        (((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm x) fun x ↦ v) *
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm x) fun x ↦ w)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.radial_identity
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (z : ℝ) (hz : z ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1) :
  D.b z + D.c z / D.b z * (1 - z ^ 2) = D.a z ^ 2 / D.b z
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.metric_inner
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (v w : TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  ((D.metric.inner x) v) w =
    D.b (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
        ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round.inner x) v) w +
      D.c (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) / D.b (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
        (((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm x) fun x ↦ v) *
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm x) fun x ↦ w)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.constant_metric_inner (r : ℝ) (hr : 0 < r)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (v w : TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x) :
  (((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.constant r hr).metric.inner x) v) w =
    r * ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round.inner x) v) w
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.exists_metric_of_smooth_positive_balanced_profile (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) a)
  (haPos : ∀ z ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a z) (hBalance : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.balance a = 0) :
  ∃ D g, D.a = a ∧ D.b (-1) = a (-1) ∧ D.b 1 = a 1 ∧ g = D.metric
LinearMap.det_smul_add_rankOne_gram_fin_two.{u_1, u_2} {R : Type u_1} {V : Type u_2} [CommRing R] [AddCommGroup V]
  [Module R V] (g : V →ₗ[R] V →ₗ[R] R) (hsym : ∀ (v w : V), (g v) w = (g w) v) (B : Module.Basis (Fin 2) R V) (Z : V)
  (b q : R) :
  (Matrix.of fun i j ↦ b * (g (B i)) (B j) + q * ((g Z) (B i) * (g Z) (B j))).det =
    b * (b + q * (g Z) Z) * (Matrix.of fun i j ↦ (g (B i)) (B j)).det
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.round_grad_sphereHeight_inner_self
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round.inner x)
        (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.gradFun RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x))
      (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.gradFun RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) =
    1 - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.det_chartGramMatrix_metric
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (α x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (hx :
    x ∈
      (trivializationAt (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) (TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))))
          α).baseSet) :
  (DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.Coordinates.chartGramMatrix D.metric α x).det =
    D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) ^ 2 *
      (DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.Coordinates.chartGramMatrix
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round α x).det
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.chartDensity_metric
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (α x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (hx :
    x ∈
      (trivializationAt (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) (TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))))
          α).baseSet) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.chartDensity D.metric α x =
    D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
      DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.chartDensity RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round
        α x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.volume_metric_eq_withDensity
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) D.metric =
    (DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
          (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round).withDensity
      fun x ↦ ENNReal.ofReal (D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.integral_volume_metric
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (F : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere → ℝ) :
  ∫ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
      F
        x ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
        (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
        D.metric =
    ∫ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
      D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
        F
          x ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
        (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.integral_round_height {F : ℝ → ℝ} (hF : ContinuousOn F (Set.Icc (-1) 1)) :
  ∫ (p : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
      F
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight
          p) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
        (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round =
    2 * Real.pi * ∫ (z : ℝ) in -1..1, F z
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.integral_height_metric
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) {F : ℝ → ℝ} (hF : ContinuousOn F (Set.Icc (-1) 1)) :
  ∫ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
      F
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight
          x) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
        (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
        D.metric =
    2 * Real.pi * ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, D.a v * F v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.metricScalarAt_eq_radialDiagonalJet.{u_1, u_2} {H : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) H} [I.Boundaryless] {M : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (α x : M)
  (hx : x ∈ DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Connection.chartLeviCivitaGoodSet α) (E G E₁ G₁ E₂ G₂ : ℝ)
  (hval :
    ∀ (i j : Fin (Module.finrank ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))),
      (DifferentialGeometry.Analysis.jet2 (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.chartGramPi g α)
              (↑(extChartAt I α) x)).1
          i j =
        if i = 0 ∧ j = 0 then E else if i = 1 ∧ j = 1 then G else 0)
  (hd1 :
    ∀ (m i j : Fin (Module.finrank ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))),
      (DifferentialGeometry.Analysis.jet2 (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.chartGramPi g α)
                (↑(extChartAt I α) x)).2.1
          ((DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.Coordinates.chartModelBasis (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) m) i j =
        if m = 0 then if i = 0 ∧ j = 0 then E₁ else if i = 1 ∧ j = 1 then G₁ else 0 else 0)
  (hd2 :
    ∀ (m n i j : Fin (Module.finrank ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))),
      ((DifferentialGeometry.Analysis.jet2 (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.chartGramPi g α)
                  (↑(extChartAt I α) x)).2.2
            ((DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.Coordinates.chartModelBasis (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) m))
          ((DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.Coordinates.chartModelBasis (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) n) i j =
        if m = 0 ∧ n = 0 then if i = 0 ∧ j = 0 then E₂ else if i = 1 ∧ j = 1 then G₂ else 0 else 0) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x =
    2 * ((-G₂ / (2 * G) + G₁ ^ 2 / (4 * G ^ 2) + E₁ * G₁ / (4 * E * G)) / E)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.radialCoefficient : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData →
  ℝ → ℝ :=
fun D v ↦ D.a v ^ 2 / RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.radialCoefficient_contDiffOn
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) :
  ContDiffOn ℝ (↑⊤) D.radialCoefficient (Set.Ioo (-1) 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.deriv_warp_hasDerivAt
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (v : ℝ) :
  HasDerivAt (deriv (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a)) (-2 * D.a v - 2 * v * deriv D.a v) v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.radialCoefficient_hasDerivAt
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (v : ℝ) (hv : v ∈ Set.Ioo (-1) 1) :
  HasDerivAt D.radialCoefficient
    ((2 * D.a v * deriv D.a v * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a v + 2 * v * D.a v ^ 3) /
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a v ^ 2)
    v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.radial_curvature_identity
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (v : ℝ) (hv : v ∈ Set.Ioo (-1) 1) :
  2 *
      ((-deriv (deriv (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a)) v /
              (2 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a v) +
            deriv (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a) v ^ 2 /
              (4 * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a v ^ 2) +
          deriv D.radialCoefficient v * deriv (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a) v /
            (4 * D.radialCoefficient v * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a v)) /
        D.radialCoefficient v) =
    2 / D.a v
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderDomain : TopologicalSpace.Opens (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) :=
{ carrier := {q | q.ofLp 0 ∈ Set.Ioo (-1) 1}, is_open' := RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderDomain._proof_3 }
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap : ↥RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderDomain →
  ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere :=
fun q ↦ ⟨RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderAmbient✝ ↑q, ⋯⟩
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap_coe (q : ↥RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderDomain) :
  ↑(RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q) =
    !₂[√(1 - (↑q).ofLp 0 ^ 2) * Real.cos ((↑q).ofLp 1), √(1 - (↑q).ofLp 0 ^ 2) * Real.sin ((↑q).ofLp 1), (↑q).ofLp 0]
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight_cylinderMap (q : ↥RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderDomain) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q) = (↑q).ofLp 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap_contMDiff :
  ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤)
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap_dIncl_mfderiv (q : ↥RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderDomain)
  (V : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) :
  (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q))
      ((mfderiv% RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q) V) =
    !₂[-(↑q).ofLp 0 / √(1 - (↑q).ofLp 0 ^ 2) * Real.cos ((↑q).ofLp 1) * V.ofLp 0 -
        √(1 - (↑q).ofLp 0 ^ 2) * Real.sin ((↑q).ofLp 1) * V.ofLp 1,
      -(↑q).ofLp 0 / √(1 - (↑q).ofLp 0 ^ 2) * Real.sin ((↑q).ofLp 1) * V.ofLp 0 +
        √(1 - (↑q).ofLp 0 ^ 2) * Real.cos ((↑q).ofLp 1) * V.ofLp 1,
      V.ofLp 0]
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap_round_inner (q : ↥RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderDomain)
  (V W : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) :
  ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.HeightMetricCoefficients.round.inner
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q))
        ((mfderiv% RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q) V))
      ((mfderiv% RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q) W) =
    1 / (1 - (↑q).ofLp 0 ^ 2) * V.ofLp 0 * W.ofLp 0 + (1 - (↑q).ofLp 0 ^ 2) * V.ofLp 1 * W.ofLp 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap_mfderiv_injective (q : ↥RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderDomain) :
  Function.Injective ⇑(mfderiv% RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.cylinderMap_warp_pos
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (q : ↥RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderDomain) :
  0 < RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a ((↑q).ofLp 0)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.cylinderMap_metric_inner
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (q : ↥RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderDomain)
  (V W : EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) :
  ((D.metric.inner (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q))
        ((mfderiv% RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q) V))
      ((mfderiv% RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q) W) =
    D.a ((↑q).ofLp 0) ^ 2 / RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a ((↑q).ofLp 0) * V.ofLp 0 *
        W.ofLp 0 +
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp D.a ((↑q).ofLp 0) * V.ofLp 1 * W.ofLp 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.range_cylinderMap :
  Set.range RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap =
    {p | RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight p ∈ Set.Ioo (-1) 1}
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.denseRange_cylinderMap : DenseRange RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.metricScalarAt_cylinderMap
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (q : ↥RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderDomain) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt D.metric (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.cylinderMap q) =
    2 / D.a ((↑q).ofLp 0)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.metricScalarAt_metric
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (p : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt D.metric p =
    2 / D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight p)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.sectionalCurvature_metric
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (p : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
  (v w : TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) p) (hvw : LinearIndependent ℝ ![v, w]) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Riemannian.sectionalCurvature D.metric p v w =
    1 / D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight p)
DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.TotalNabla0SRealizes.unique.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] {s : ℕ}
  {cov : CovariantDerivative I E (TangentSpace I)} {A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s}
  {B C : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) (s + 1)}
  (hB : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.TotalNabla0SRealizes s cov A B)
  (hC : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.TotalNabla0SRealizes s cov A C) : B = C
DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.CanonicalSpatialDerivs0S.unique.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]

  {A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s}
  (D₁ D₂ : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.CanonicalSpatialDerivs0S cov A) : D₁ = D₂
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.canonicalDerivatives.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : {E : Type u_1} →
  [inst : NormedAddCommGroup E] →
    [inst_1 : NormedSpace ℝ E] →
      [inst_2 : FiniteDimensional ℝ E] →
        {H : Type u_2} →
          [inst_3 : TopologicalSpace H] →
            {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} →
              {M : Type u_3} →
                [inst_4 : TopologicalSpace M] →
                  [inst_5 : ChartedSpace H M] →
                    [inst_6 : IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] →
                      [T2Space M] →
                        {s : ℕ} →
                          (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) →
                            (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) →
                              DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.CanonicalSpatialDerivs0S
                                (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricCov g) A :=
fun {E} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H} [TopologicalSpace H] {I} {M}
    [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] {s} g A ↦
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.CanonicalSpatialDerivs0S.ofSmoothConnection
    (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricCov g) ⋯ A
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.canonicalDerivatives_nablaA.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}

  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.canonicalDerivatives g A).nablaA =
    DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g A
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.canonicalDerivatives_nabla2A.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.canonicalDerivatives_unique.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.canonicalDerivatives_nabla2A_add.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}

  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.canonicalDerivatives g (A + B)).nabla2A =
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.canonicalDerivatives g A).nabla2A +
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.canonicalDerivatives g B).nabla2A
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.canonicalDerivatives_nabla2A_smul.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]

  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) :
  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.canonicalDerivatives g (c • A)).nabla2A =
    c • (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.canonicalDerivatives g A).nabla2A
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.canonicalDerivatives_nabla2OneFormRealizesAt.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}

    ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g A)) x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.roughLap0SField_add.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
