RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipationPairingDensity_rotationalAverage
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairingDensity D.metric
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h) k x =
    (2 * Real.pi)⁻¹ *
      ∫ (θ : ℝ) in 0..2 * Real.pi,
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairingDensity D.metric
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation θ)
            h)
          k x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipationPairing_rotationalAverage
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing D.metric
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h) k =
    (2 * Real.pi)⁻¹ *
      ∫ (θ : ℝ) in 0..2 * Real.pi,
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing D.metric
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation θ)
            h)
          k
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipationPairing_rotationalAverage_selfAdjoint
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing D.metric
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h) k =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing D.metric h
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage k)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipationPairing_average_sub_average
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h k : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipationPairing D.metric
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h) (k - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage k) =
    0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_haar_split
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric h =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h) +
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric
        (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.axisRotation : Circle → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 3) ≃ₗᵢ[ℝ] EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 3) :=
fun z ↦
  let __LinearEquiv :=
    { toFun := RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.axisRotationFun✝ z, map_add' := ⋯, map_smul' := ⋯,
      invFun := RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.axisRotationFun✝ z⁻¹, left_inv := ⋯, right_inv := ⋯ };
  { toLinearEquiv := __LinearEquiv, norm_map' := ⋯ }
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.circleSphereDiffeo : Circle →
  Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
    ↑RotationalSphere ↑RotationalSphere ↑⊤ :=
fun z ↦ DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.sphereDiffeo (axisRotation z)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation : ℝ →
  Diffeomorph (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
    ↑RotationalSphere ↑RotationalSphere ↑⊤ :=
fun theta ↦ circleSphereDiffeo (Circle.exp theta)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.tensorIntervalIntegral.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : {E : Type u_1} →
  [inst : NormedAddCommGroup E] →
    [inst_1 : NormedSpace ℝ E] →
      [inst_2 : FiniteDimensional ℝ E] →
        {H : Type u_2} →
          [inst_3 : TopologicalSpace H] →
            {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} →
              {M : Type u_3} →
                [inst_4 : TopologicalSpace M] →
                  [inst_5 : ChartedSpace H M] →
                    [inst_6 : IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] →
                      [I.Boundaryless] →
                        {s : ℕ} →
                          (A : ℝ → DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) →
                            (ContMDiff ((modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ).prod I)
                                (I.prod
                                  (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SModel s ℝ E)))
                                ↑⊤ fun p ↦ ⟨p.2, (A p.1) p.2⟩) →
                              ℝ → ℝ → DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s :=
fun {E} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H} [TopologicalSpace H] {I} {M}
    [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [I.Boundaryless] {s} A hA a b ↦
  { toFun := fun x ↦ ∫ (t : ℝ) in a..b, (A t) x, contMDiff_toFun := ⋯ }
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage : {s : ℕ} →
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s → DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s :=
fun {s} A ↦
  (2 * Real.pi)⁻¹ • tensorIntervalIntegral (fun θ ↦ diffeomorphTensorPullback (angleRotation θ) A) ⋯ 0 (2 * Real.pi)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_contDiff_mul_eq_on_Icc (a b : ℝ) (hab : a ≤ b) (c p : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hc : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) c) (hp : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) p) (hne : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc a b, c x ≠ 0) :
  ∃ r, ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r ∧ ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc a b, c x * r x = p x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_contDiff_comp_sq_of_even (e : ℝ → ℝ) (he : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) e)
  (heven : Function.Even e) : ∃ E, ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) E ∧ ∀ (x : ℝ), e x = E (x ^ 2)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_contDiff_mul_comp_sq_of_odd (p : ℝ → ℝ) (hp : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) p)
  (hodd : Function.Odd p) : ∃ P, ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) P ∧ ∀ (x : ℝ), p x = x * P (x ^ 2)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.planeRotate : ℝ → ℝ → ℝ × ℝ → ℝ × ℝ :=
fun c s p ↦ (c * p.1 - s * p.2, s * p.1 + c * p.2)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.IsRotationCovectorPair : (ℝ × ℝ → ℝ) → (ℝ × ℝ → ℝ) → Prop :=
fun P Q ↦
  ∀ (c s : ℝ),
    c ^ 2 + s ^ 2 = 1 →
      ∀ (p : ℝ × ℝ), P (planeRotate c s p) = c * P p - s * Q p ∧ Q (planeRotate c s p) = s * P p + c * Q p
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_smooth_radial_tangential_coefficients (P Q : ℝ × ℝ → ℝ) (hP : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) P)
  (hQ : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) Q) (hcov : IsRotationCovectorPair P Q) :
  ∃ A B,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) A ∧
      ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) B ∧
        ∀ (x y : ℝ),
          P (x, y) = x * A (x ^ 2 + y ^ 2) - y * B (x ^ 2 + y ^ 2) ∧
            Q (x, y) = y * A (x ^ 2 + y ^ 2) + x * B (x ^ 2 + y ^ 2)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.stereoDenominator : ℝ × ℝ → ℝ :=
fun p ↦ p.1 ^ 2 + p.2 ^ 2 + 4
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.stereoDenominator_pos (p : ℝ × ℝ) : 0 < stereoDenominator p
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.stereoAmbient : ℝ → ℝ × ℝ → EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 3) :=
fun epsilon p ↦
  !₂[4 * p.1 / stereoDenominator p, 4 * p.2 / stereoDenominator p,
    epsilon * (4 - p.1 ^ 2 - p.2 ^ 2) / stereoDenominator p]
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.stereoPoint : (epsilon : ℝ) → epsilon ^ 2 = 1 → ℝ × ℝ → ↑RotationalSphere :=
fun epsilon he p ↦ ⟨stereoAmbient epsilon p, ⋯⟩
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.stereoPoint_contMDiff (epsilon : ℝ) (he : epsilon ^ 2 = 1) :
  ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (ℝ × ℝ)) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤)
    (stereoPoint epsilon he)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.stereoPoint_coe (epsilon : ℝ) (he : epsilon ^ 2 = 1) (p : ℝ × ℝ) :
  ↑(stereoPoint epsilon he p) = stereoAmbient epsilon p
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight_stereoPoint (epsilon : ℝ) (he : epsilon ^ 2 = 1) (p : ℝ × ℝ) :
  sphereHeight (stereoPoint epsilon he p) = epsilon * (4 - p.1 ^ 2 - p.2 ^ 2) / stereoDenominator p
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.stereoPoint_height_denominator (epsilon : ℝ) (he : epsilon ^ 2 = 1) (p : ℝ × ℝ) :
  1 + epsilon * sphereHeight (stereoPoint epsilon he p) = 8 / stereoDenominator p
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.stereoPoint_planeRotate (epsilon : ℝ) (he : epsilon ^ 2 = 1) (z : Circle) (p : ℝ × ℝ) :
  stereoPoint epsilon he ((↑z).re * p.1 - (↑z).im * p.2, (↑z).im * p.1 + (↑z).re * p.2) =
    (circleSphereDiffeo z) (stereoPoint epsilon he p)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.stereoInversePoint : ℝ → ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ × ℝ :=
fun epsilon x ↦ (2 * (↑x).ofLp 0 / (1 + epsilon * sphereHeight x), 2 * (↑x).ofLp 1 / (1 + epsilon * sphereHeight x))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.stereoInversePoint_stereoPoint (epsilon : ℝ) (he : epsilon ^ 2 = 1) (p : ℝ × ℝ) :
  stereoInversePoint epsilon (stereoPoint epsilon he p) = p
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.stereoPoint_inverse (epsilon : ℝ) (he : epsilon ^ 2 = 1) (x : ↑RotationalSphere)
  (hx : 1 + epsilon * sphereHeight x ≠ 0) : stereoPoint epsilon he (stereoInversePoint epsilon x) = x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.stereoPoint_dIncl_mfderiv (epsilon : ℝ) (he : epsilon ^ 2 = 1) (p v : ℝ × ℝ) :
  (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl (stereoPoint epsilon he p)) ((mfderiv% (stereoPoint epsilon he) p) v) =
    !₂[4 / stereoDenominator p * v.1 - 8 * p.1 / stereoDenominator p ^ 2 * (p.1 * v.1 + p.2 * v.2),
      4 / stereoDenominator p * v.2 - 8 * p.2 / stereoDenominator p ^ 2 * (p.1 * v.1 + p.2 * v.2),
      -16 * epsilon / stereoDenominator p ^ 2 * (p.1 * v.1 + p.2 * v.2)]
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.stereoPoint_mfderiv_injective (epsilon : ℝ) (he : epsilon ^ 2 = 1) (p : ℝ × ℝ) :
  Function.Injective ⇑(mfderiv% (stereoPoint epsilon he) p)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.stereoPoint_mfderiv_surjective (epsilon : ℝ) (he : epsilon ^ 2 = 1) (p : ℝ × ℝ) :
  Function.Surjective ⇑(mfderiv% (stereoPoint epsilon he) p)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.heightOneForm_stereoPoint_mfderiv (epsilon : ℝ) (he : epsilon ^ 2 = 1) (p v : ℝ × ℝ) :
  ((heightOneForm (stereoPoint epsilon he p)) fun x ↦ (mfderiv% (stereoPoint epsilon he) p) v) =
    -16 * epsilon * (p.1 * v.1 + p.2 * v.2) / stereoDenominator p ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereAzimuthalOneForm_stereoPoint_mfderiv (epsilon : ℝ) (he : epsilon ^ 2 = 1)
  (p v : ℝ × ℝ) :
  ((sphereAzimuthalOneForm (stereoPoint epsilon he p)) fun x ↦ (mfderiv% (stereoPoint epsilon he) p) v) =
    16 * (p.1 * v.2 - p.2 * v.1) / stereoDenominator p ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.exists_smooth_stereographic_coefficients
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection)
  (hinv : ∀ (z : Circle), diffeomorphTensorPullback (circleSphereDiffeo z) h = h) (epsilon : ℝ) (he : epsilon ^ 2 = 1) :
  ∃ A B,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) A ∧
      ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) B ∧
        ∀ (p v : ℝ × ℝ),
          ((h (stereoPoint epsilon he p)) fun x ↦ (mfderiv% (stereoPoint epsilon he) p) v) =
            A (p.1 ^ 2 + p.2 ^ 2) * (p.1 * v.1 + p.2 * v.2) + B (p.1 ^ 2 + p.2 ^ 2) * (p.1 * v.2 - p.2 * v.1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.exists_smooth_height_azimuthal_profiles
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection)
  (hinv : ∀ (z : Circle), diffeomorphTensorPullback (circleSphereDiffeo z) h = h) :
  ∃ P Q,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) P ∧
      ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) Q ∧
        ∀ (x : ↑RotationalSphere) (V : TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x),
          ((h x) fun x ↦ V) =
            (P (sphereHeight x) * (heightOneForm x) fun x ↦ V) +
              Q (sphereHeight x) * (sphereAzimuthalOneForm x) fun x ↦ V
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.exists_smooth_zonal_decomposition_of_rotationInvariant
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection)
  (hinv :
    ∀ (z : Circle),
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.circleSphereDiffeo z)
          h =
        h) :
  ∃ r s, ∃ (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s), h = D.meridionalOneForm r hr + D.azimuthalOneForm s hs
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.exists_smooth_zonal_decomposition_rotationalAverage
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  ∃ r s,
    ∃ (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s),
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h = D.meridionalOneForm r hr + D.azimuthalOneForm s hs
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.exists_haar_zonal_dissipation
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  ∃ r s,
    ∃ (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s),
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h = D.meridionalOneForm r hr + D.azimuthalOneForm s hs ∧
        RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric h =
          2 * Real.pi * (D.meridionalAction r + D.meridionalAction s) +
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric
              (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormTrace.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : {E : Type u_1} →
  [inst : NormedAddCommGroup E] →
    [inst_1 : NormedSpace ℝ E] →
      [inst_2 : FiniteDimensional ℝ E] →
        {H : Type u_2} →
          [inst_3 : TopologicalSpace H] →
            {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} →
              {M : Type u_3} →
                [inst_4 : TopologicalSpace M] →
                  [inst_5 : ChartedSpace H M] →
                    [inst_6 : IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] →
                      [T2Space M] →
                        DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M →
                          DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection → M → ℝ :=
fun {E} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H} [TopologicalSpace H] {I} {M}
    [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] g h x ↦
  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt g
    ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h) x)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormCurl.{u_1, u_2, u_3} : {E : Type u_1} →
  [inst : NormedAddCommGroup E] →
    [inst_1 : NormedSpace ℝ E] →
      [inst_2 : FiniteDimensional ℝ E] →
        {H : Type u_2} →
          [inst_3 : TopologicalSpace H] →
            {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} →
              {M : Type u_3} →
                [inst_4 : TopologicalSpace M] →
                  [inst_5 : ChartedSpace H M] →
                    [inst_6 : IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] →
                      [T2Space M] →
                        DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M →
                          DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.TwoTensorSection →
                            DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection → M → ℝ :=
fun {E} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H} [TopologicalSpace H] {I} {M}
    [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] g Ω h x ↦
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 2 (Ω x) ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h) x)

  ContMDiff I (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤) (oneFormCurl g Ω h)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.inner0S_eq_sum_orthonormal.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] {Idx : Type u_4} [Fintype Idx]
  [DecidableEq Idx] (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (x : M) (s : ℕ)
  (B : Module.Basis Idx ℝ (TangentSpace I x)) (hB : ∀ (i j : Idx), ((g.inner x) (B i)) (B j) = if i = j then 1 else 0)
  (T U : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SSpace s I x) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x s T U = ∑ v, (T fun i ↦ B (v i)) * U fun i ↦ B (v i)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.normSq0S_eq_sum_sq_orthonormal.{u_1, u_2, u_3, u_4} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  {Idx : Type u_4} [Fintype Idx] [DecidableEq Idx] (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (x : M) (s : ℕ)
  (B : Module.Basis Idx ℝ (TangentSpace I x)) (hB : ∀ (i j : Idx), ((g.inner x) (B i)) (B j) = if i = j then 1 else 0)
  (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SSpace s I x) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x s T = ∑ v, (T fun i ↦ B (v i)) ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.twoTensor_eq_ahlforsPart_add_trace_add_curl.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E = 2) (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2) (x : M)
  (Ω : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SSpace 2 I x)
  (hAlt :
    ∀ (v w : TangentSpace I x),
      Ω (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 v w) = -Ω (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 w v))
  (hUnit : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2 Ω = 2) :
  T x =
    (ahlforsPart g T) x +
        (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt g (T x) / 2) •
          DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTensor0S g x +
      (DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 2 Ω (T x) / 2) • Ω
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.normSq0S_twoTensor_eq_ahlforsPart_trace_curl.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M]
  (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E = 2) (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2) (x : M)
  (Ω : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SSpace 2 I x)
  (hAlt :
    ∀ (v w : TangentSpace I x),
      Ω (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 v w) = -Ω (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 w v))
  (hUnit : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2 Ω = 2) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2 (T x) =
    DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2 ((ahlforsPart g T) x) +
      (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt g (T x) ^ 2 +
          DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 2 Ω (T x) ^ 2) /
        2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.inner0S_ahlforsPart_self.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E = 2)
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2)
  (x : M) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 2 ((ahlforsPart g T) x) (T x) =
    DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2 ((ahlforsPart g T) x)

    [inst_1 : NormedSpace ℝ E] →
      [inst_2 : FiniteDimensional ℝ E] →

              {M : Type u_3} →
                [inst_4 : TopologicalSpace M] →
                  [inst_5 : ChartedSpace H M] →

                      [CompactSpace M] →
                        {s : ℕ} →
                          (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) →
                            DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s →
                              DifferentialGeometry.Integral.L2.SmoothCcTensor g 0 s :=
fun {E} [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H} [TopologicalSpace H] {I} {M}

  { toSection := DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField.toTensorRSField (↑⊤) T, hasCompactSupport := ⋯ }

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.integral_inner0S_metricNabla0S_eq_neg_covDiv0SField.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.metricNabla0S_commutator.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] [I.Boundaryless] {s : ℕ}
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (S : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s)
  (x : M) (X Y : TangentSpace I x) (slots : Fin s → TangentSpace I x) :
  ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g S)) x)
        (Fin.cons X (Fin.cons Y slots)) -
      ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g S)) x)
        (Fin.cons Y (Fin.cons X slots)) =
    -∑ q,
        (S x)
          (Function.update slots q
            ((((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.riemannOp
                    (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Connection.LeviCivita g) x)
                  X)
                Y)
              (slots q)))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.covDiv0SField_gradSlotSwap_commutator_of_finrank_eq_two.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  [I.Boundaryless] (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E = 2) (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (S : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2) :
  DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.covDiv0SField g
      (DifferentialGeometry.MultilinearSection.domDomCongr (↑⊤) (Equiv.swap 0 1)
        (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g S)) =
    DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.covDiv0SField g S) +
      DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.tensor0SFieldSmulByFun (↑⊤)
        (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g) ⋯ (ahlforsPart g S)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneForm_secondDerivative_skew_pairing.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  [I.Boundaryless] (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E = 2) (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 1) (x : M) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 3
        ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h))
          x) -
      DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 3
        (((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h))
              x).domDomCongr
          (Equiv.swap 0 1))
        ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h))
          x) =
    (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x / 2) ^ 2 *
      DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1 (h x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.differential1FormFun_metricTrace_apply.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2)
  (x : M) (X : TangentSpace I x) :
  ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.differential1FormFun
        (fun y ↦ DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt g (T y)) x)
      fun x ↦ X) =
    DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt g
      (((DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.tensor0SCurry 2 x)
          ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g T) x))
        X)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.differential1FormFun_inner0S_two_apply.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (S T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2)
  (x : M) (X : TangentSpace I x) :
  ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.differential1FormFun
        (fun y ↦ DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g y 2 (S y) (T y)) x)
      fun x ↦ X) =
    DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 2
        (((DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.tensor0SCurry 2 x)
            ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g S) x))
          X)
        (T x) +
      DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 2 (S x)
        (((DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.tensor0SCurry 2 x)
            ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g T) x))
          X)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.metricNabla0S_ahlforsPart_apply.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E = 2) (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2) (x : M) (X Y Z : TangentSpace I x) :
  ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g (ahlforsPart g T)) x)
      (Fin.cons X (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 Y Z)) =
    (((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g T) x)
            (Fin.cons X (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 Y Z)) +
          ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g T) x)
            (Fin.cons X (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 Z Y))) /
        2 -
      ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.differential1FormFun
              (fun y ↦ DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt g (T y)) x)
            fun x ↦ X) /
          2 *
        ((g.inner x) Y) Z
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.normSq0S_metricNabla0S_twoTensor_split.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E = 2) (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (T Ω : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.TwoTensorSection)
  (hAlt :
    ∀ (x : M) (v w : TangentSpace I x),
      (Ω x) (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 v w) =
        -(Ω x) (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 w v))
  (hUnit : ∀ (x : M), DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2 (Ω x) = 2) (x : M) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 3 ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g T) x) =
    DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 3
        ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g (ahlforsPart g T)) x) +
      (DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1
            (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.differential1FormFun
              (fun y ↦ DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt g (T y)) x) +
          DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1
            (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.differential1FormFun
              (fun y ↦ DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g y 2 (Ω y) (T y)) x)) /
        2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.integral_inner0S_gradSlotSwap.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  [I.Boundaryless] [BoundarylessManifold I M] (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E = 2)
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (S : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.TwoTensorSection) :
  ∫ (x : M),
      DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g x 3
        (((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g S) x).domDomCongr (Equiv.swap 0 1))
        ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g S)
          x) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure I M g =
    ∫ (x : M),
        DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1
          ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.covDiv0SField g S)
            x) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure I M g -
      ∫ (x : M),
        DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x *
          DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2
            ((ahlforsPart g S) x) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure I M g
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation_scalarization.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M] [BoundarylessManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M)
  (Ω : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.TwoTensorSection)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection)
  (hAlt :
    ∀ (x : M) (v w : TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) x),
      (Ω x) (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 v w) =
        -(Ω x) (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 w v))
  (hUnit : ∀ (x : M), DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2 (Ω x) = 2) :
  oneFormDissipation g h =
    ∫ (x : M),
          DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 3
            ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g
                (ahlforsPart g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h)))
              x) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
            (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M g +
        3 *
          ∫ (x : M),
            DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x / 2 *
              DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2
                ((ahlforsPart g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h))
                  x) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
              (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M g +
      1 / 2 *
        (∫ (x : M),
            DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1
                (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.differential1FormFun (oneFormTrace g h) x) -
              DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x / 2 *
                oneFormTrace g h x ^
                  2 ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
              (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M g +
          ∫ (x : M),
            DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1
                (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.differential1FormFun (oneFormCurl g Ω h) x) -
              DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x / 2 *
                oneFormCurl g Ω h x ^
                  2 ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
              (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M g)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_scalarization
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric h =
    ∫ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
          DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric x 3
            ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S D.metric
                (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart D.metric
                  (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S D.metric h)))
              x) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
            (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
            D.metric +
        3 *
          ∫ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
            1 / D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
              DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric x 2
                ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart D.metric
                    (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S D.metric h))
                  x) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
              (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
              D.metric +
      1 / 2 *
        (∫ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
            DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric x 1
                (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.differential1FormFun
                  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormTrace D.metric h) x) -
              1 / D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormTrace D.metric h x ^
                  2 ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
              (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
              D.metric +
          ∫ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
            DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric x 1
                (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.differential1FormFun
                  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormCurl D.metric D.areaForm h) x) -
              1 / D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormCurl D.metric D.areaForm h x ^
                  2 ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
              (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
              D.metric)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.inner0S_diffeomorphTensorPullback.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (f : Diffeomorph I I M M ↑⊤) {s : ℕ}
  (A B : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) (x : M) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric g f) x s
      ((diffeomorphTensorPullback f A) x) ((diffeomorphTensorPullback f B) x) =
    DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.inner0S g (f x) s (A (f x)) (B (f x))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.metricTracePair0SAt_diffeomorphTensorPullback.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (f : Diffeomorph I I M M ↑⊤)
  (A : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) 2) (x : M) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric g f)
      ((diffeomorphTensorPullback f A) x) =
    DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt g (A (f x))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormTrace_diffeomorphTensorPullback.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (f : Diffeomorph I I M M ↑⊤)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) (x : M) :
  oneFormTrace (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric g f) (diffeomorphTensorPullback f h) x =
    oneFormTrace g h (f x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormCurl_diffeomorphTensorPullback.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (f : Diffeomorph I I M M ↑⊤)
  (Ω : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.TwoTensorSection)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) (x : M) :
  oneFormCurl (DifferentialGeometry.Diffeomorph.pullbackMetric g f) (diffeomorphTensorPullback f Ω)
      (diffeomorphTensorPullback f h) x =
    oneFormCurl g Ω h (f x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback_roundSphereAreaForm_circleSphereDiffeo (z : Circle) :
  diffeomorphTensorPullback (circleSphereDiffeo z) roundSphereAreaForm = roundSphereAreaForm
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback_roundSphereAreaForm_angleRotation (θ : ℝ) :
  diffeomorphTensorPullback (angleRotation θ) roundSphereAreaForm = roundSphereAreaForm
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage (u : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ) (x : ↑RotationalSphere) : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage_contMDiff {u : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ}
  (hu : ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤) u) :
  ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤)
    (rotationalScalarAverage u)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage_add {u v : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ} (hu : Continuous u)
  (hv : Continuous v) : rotationalScalarAverage (u + v) = rotationalScalarAverage u + rotationalScalarAverage v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage_sub {u v : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ} (hu : Continuous u)
  (hv : Continuous v) : rotationalScalarAverage (u - v) = rotationalScalarAverage u - rotationalScalarAverage v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage_smul (c : ℝ) (u : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ) :
  rotationalScalarAverage (c • u) = c • rotationalScalarAverage u
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage_const (c : ℝ) : (rotationalScalarAverage fun x ↦ c) = fun x ↦ c
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage_zero : rotationalScalarAverage 0 = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage_one : rotationalScalarAverage 1 = 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage_angleRotation (u : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ) (φ : ℝ)
  (x : ↑RotationalSphere) : rotationalScalarAverage u ((angleRotation φ) x) = rotationalScalarAverage u x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage_comp_angleRotation (u : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ) (φ : ℝ) :
  (rotationalScalarAverage fun x ↦ u ((angleRotation φ) x)) = rotationalScalarAverage u
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage_eq_self_of_invariant (u : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ)
  (hu : ∀ (θ : ℝ) (x : ↑RotationalSphere), u ((angleRotation θ) x) = u x) : rotationalScalarAverage u = u
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage_idempotent (u : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ) :
  rotationalScalarAverage (rotationalScalarAverage u) = rotationalScalarAverage u
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage_eq_at_pole (u : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ) (x : ↑RotationalSphere)
  (hx : sphereHeight x ^ 2 = 1) : rotationalScalarAverage u x = u x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage_eq_zero_at_pole {u : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ}
  (hu : rotationalScalarAverage u = 0) (x : ↑RotationalSphere) (hx : sphereHeight x ^ 2 = 1) : u x = 0

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.metricTracePair0SAt_rotationalAverage
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.TwoTensorSection)

  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.metricTracePair0SAt D.metric

  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.TwoTensorSection)

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormTrace_angleRotation
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (θ : ℝ)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormTrace D.metric
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation θ) h)
      x =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormTrace D.metric h ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation θ) x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormCurl_angleRotation
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (θ : ℝ)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormCurl D.metric D.areaForm
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation θ) h)
      x =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormCurl D.metric D.areaForm h
      ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation θ) x)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormTrace_rotationalAverage
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormTrace D.metric (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormTrace D.metric h)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormCurl_rotationalAverage
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormCurl D.metric D.areaForm
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormCurl D.metric D.areaForm h)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.rotationalScalarAverage_oneFormTrace_remainder
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)

  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormTrace D.metric
        (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h)) =
    0

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormTrace_remainder_eq_zero_at_pole
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integral_sq_le_deriv_sq_of_periodic_mean_zero (f : ℝ → ℝ) (hf : ContDiff ℝ 1 f)
  (hperiod : f (2 * Real.pi) = f 0) (hmean : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..2 * Real.pi, f x = 0) :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..2 * Real.pi, f x ^ 2 ≤ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..2 * Real.pi, deriv f x ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.contDiff_deriv_fst {q : ℝ × ℝ → ℝ} {m n : WithTop ℕ∞} (hq : ContDiff ℝ n q)
  (hmn : m + 1 ≤ n) : ContDiff ℝ m fun x ↦ deriv (fun t ↦ q (t, x.2)) x.1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.contDiff_deriv_snd {q : ℝ × ℝ → ℝ} {m n : WithTop ℕ∞} (hq : ContDiff ℝ n q)
  (hmn : m + 1 ≤ n) : ContDiff ℝ m fun x ↦ deriv (fun t ↦ q (x.1, t)) x.2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_pos_polar_endpoint_bounds (q : ℝ × ℝ → ℝ) (hq : ContDiff ℝ 2 q)
  (hzero : ∀ (θ : ℝ), q (0, θ) = 0) (hpi : ∀ (θ : ℝ), q (Real.pi, θ) = 0) (a b : ℝ) :
  ∃ C > 0,
    ∀ s ∈ Set.Icc 0 Real.pi,
      ∀ θ ∈ Set.Icc a b,
        |q (s, θ)| ≤ C * Real.sin s ∧ |deriv (fun t ↦ q (t, θ)) s| ≤ C ∧ |deriv (fun t ↦ q (s, t)) θ| ≤ C * Real.sin s
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.intervalIntegrable_polar_square (W y : ℝ → ℝ) (hW : ∀ s ∈ Set.Ioo 0 Real.pi, 0 < W s)
  (hy : ContDiff ℝ 1 y) (hR : IntervalIntegrable (fun s ↦ W s * deriv y s ^ 2) MeasureTheory.volume 0 Real.pi)
  (hV : IntervalIntegrable (fun s ↦ y s ^ 2 / W s) MeasureTheory.volume 0 Real.pi) :
  IntervalIntegrable (fun s ↦ (W s * deriv y s - Real.cos s * y s) ^ 2 / W s) MeasureTheory.volume 0 Real.pi
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integral_polar_square_completion (W y : ℝ → ℝ) (hW : ∀ s ∈ Set.Ioo 0 Real.pi, 0 < W s)
  (hy : ContDiff ℝ 1 y) (h0 : y 0 = 0) (hpi : y Real.pi = 0)
  (hR : IntervalIntegrable (fun s ↦ W s * deriv y s ^ 2) MeasureTheory.volume 0 Real.pi)
  (hV : IntervalIntegrable (fun s ↦ y s ^ 2 / W s) MeasureTheory.volume 0 Real.pi) :
  ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..Real.pi, W s * deriv y s ^ 2 + y s ^ 2 / W s - Real.sin s * y s ^ 2 =
    (∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..Real.pi, (W s * deriv y s - Real.cos s * y s) ^ 2 / W s) +
      ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..Real.pi, Real.sin s ^ 2 * y s ^ 2 / W s
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integral_weighted_sin_sq_le_polar_energy (W y : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hW : ∀ s ∈ Set.Ioo 0 Real.pi, 0 < W s) (hy : ContDiff ℝ 1 y) (h0 : y 0 = 0) (hpi : y Real.pi = 0)
  (hR : IntervalIntegrable (fun s ↦ W s * deriv y s ^ 2) MeasureTheory.volume 0 Real.pi)
  (hV : IntervalIntegrable (fun s ↦ y s ^ 2 / W s) MeasureTheory.volume 0 Real.pi) :
  ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..Real.pi, Real.sin s ^ 2 * y s ^ 2 / W s ≤
    ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..Real.pi, W s * deriv y s ^ 2 + y s ^ 2 / W s - Real.sin s * y s ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integrable_polar_energy_terms (q : ℝ × ℝ → ℝ) (w : ℝ → ℝ) (hq : ContDiff ℝ 2 q)
  (hzero : ∀ (θ : ℝ), q (0, θ) = 0) (hpi : ∀ (θ : ℝ), q (Real.pi, θ) = 0) (hw : Continuous w)
  (hwpos : ∀ s ∈ Set.Icc 0 Real.pi, 0 < w s) (a b : ℝ) :
  have W := fun s ↦ Real.sin s * w s;
  have μ := (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioc 0 Real.pi)).prod (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioc a b));
  MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ W x.1 * deriv (fun t ↦ q (t, x.2)) x.1 ^ 2) μ ∧
    MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ q x ^ 2 / W x.1) μ ∧
      MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ deriv (fun t ↦ q (x.1, t)) x.2 ^ 2 / W x.1) μ ∧
        MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ (W x.1 * deriv (fun t ↦ q (t, x.2)) x.1 - Real.cos x.1 * q x) ^ 2 / W x.1) μ ∧
          MeasureTheory.Integrable
              (fun x ↦ -Real.sin x.1 * q x ^ 2 + 2 * Real.cos x.1 * q x * deriv (fun t ↦ q (t, x.2)) x.1) μ ∧
            MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ Real.sin x.1 ^ 2 * q x ^ 2 / W x.1) μ ∧
              MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ Real.sin x.1 * q x ^ 2) μ ∧
                MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ Real.cos x.1 * q x ^ 2) μ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.integral_polar_energy_ge_of_angular_mean_zero (q : ℝ × ℝ → ℝ) (w : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hq : ContDiff ℝ 2 q) (hzero : ∀ (θ : ℝ), q (0, θ) = 0) (hpi : ∀ (θ : ℝ), q (Real.pi, θ) = 0)
  (hperiod : ∀ (s : ℝ), q (s, 2 * Real.pi) = q (s, 0)) (hmean : ∀ (s : ℝ), ∫ (θ : ℝ) in 0..2 * Real.pi, q (s, θ) = 0)
  (hw : Continuous w) (hwpos : ∀ s ∈ Set.Icc 0 Real.pi, 0 < w s) :
  ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..Real.pi, ∫ (θ : ℝ) in 0..2 * Real.pi, Real.sin s ^ 2 * q (s, θ) ^ 2 / (Real.sin s * w s) ≤
    ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..Real.pi,
      ∫ (θ : ℝ) in 0..2 * Real.pi,
        Real.sin s * w s * deriv (fun t ↦ q (t, θ)) s ^ 2 + deriv (fun t ↦ q (s, t)) θ ^ 2 / (Real.sin s * w s) -
          Real.sin s * q (s, θ) ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.tendsto_polar_boundary_at_endpoints (q : ℝ × ℝ → ℝ) (hq : Continuous q)
  (hzero : ∀ (θ : ℝ), q (0, θ) = 0) (hpi : ∀ (θ : ℝ), q (Real.pi, θ) = 0) (θ : ℝ) :
  Filter.Tendsto (fun s ↦ Real.cos s * q (s, θ) ^ 2) (nhds 0) (nhds 0) ∧
    Filter.Tendsto (fun s ↦ Real.cos s * q (s, θ) ^ 2) (nhds Real.pi) (nhds 0)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint (s theta : ℝ) : ↑RotationalSphere
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint_coe (s theta : ℝ) :
  ↑(polarSpherePoint s theta) = !₂[Real.sin s * Real.cos theta, Real.sin s * Real.sin theta, Real.cos s]
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight_polarSpherePoint (s theta : ℝ) :
  sphereHeight (polarSpherePoint s theta) = Real.cos s
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint_contMDiff :
  ContMDiff ((modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ).prod (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ))
    (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) ↑⊤ fun p ↦ polarSpherePoint p.1 p.2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint_periodic (s : ℝ) : Function.Periodic (polarSpherePoint s) (2 * Real.pi)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.angleRotation_polarSpherePoint (phi s theta : ℝ) :
  (angleRotation phi) (polarSpherePoint s theta) = polarSpherePoint s (phi + theta)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint_zero (theta : ℝ) :
  polarSpherePoint 0 theta = ⟨EuclideanSpace.single 2 1, ⋯⟩
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint_pi (theta : ℝ) :
  polarSpherePoint Real.pi theta = ⟨EuclideanSpace.single 2 (-1), ⋯⟩
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarRadialVelocity (s theta : ℝ) :
  TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (polarSpherePoint s theta)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarAngularVelocity (s theta : ℝ) :
  TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (polarSpherePoint s theta)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarRadialVelocity_dIncl (s theta : ℝ) :
  (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl (polarSpherePoint s theta)) (polarRadialVelocity s theta) =
    !₂[Real.cos s * Real.cos theta, Real.cos s * Real.sin theta, -Real.sin s]
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarAngularVelocity_dIncl (s theta : ℝ) :
  (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.dIncl (polarSpherePoint s theta)) (polarAngularVelocity s theta) =
    !₂[-Real.sin s * Real.sin theta, Real.sin s * Real.cos theta, 0]
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarVelocities_linearIndependent (s theta : ℝ) (hs : Real.sin s ≠ 0) :
  LinearIndependent ℝ ![polarRadialVelocity s theta, polarAngularVelocity s theta]
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarVelocities_span (s theta : ℝ) (hs : Real.sin s ≠ 0) :
  Submodule.span ℝ (Set.range ![polarRadialVelocity s theta, polarAngularVelocity s theta]) = ⊤
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarTangentBasis (s theta : ℝ) (hs : Real.sin s ≠ 0) :
  Module.Basis (Fin 2) ℝ (TangentSpace (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (polarSpherePoint s theta))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarTangentBasis_zero (s theta : ℝ) (hs : Real.sin s ≠ 0) :
  (polarTangentBasis s theta hs) 0 = polarRadialVelocity s theta
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarTangentBasis_one (s theta : ℝ) (hs : Real.sin s ≠ 0) :
  (polarTangentBasis s theta hs) 1 = polarAngularVelocity s theta
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.exists_polarSpherePoint_arccos (x : ↑RotationalSphere) :
  ∃ theta, polarSpherePoint (Real.arccos (sphereHeight x)) theta = x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.contDiff_scalar_polar {u : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ}
  (hu : ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤) u) :
  ContDiff ℝ ↑⊤ fun p ↦ u (polarSpherePoint p.1 p.2)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.differential1FormFun_polarRadialVelocity {u : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ}
  (hu : ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤) u) (s θ : ℝ) :
  ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.differential1FormFun u (polarSpherePoint s θ)) fun x ↦
      polarRadialVelocity s θ) =
    deriv (fun t ↦ u (polarSpherePoint t θ)) s
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.differential1FormFun_polarAngularVelocity {u : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ}
  (hu : ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤) u) (s θ : ℝ) :
  ((DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.differential1FormFun u (polarSpherePoint s θ)) fun x ↦
      polarAngularVelocity s θ) =
    deriv (fun t ↦ u (polarSpherePoint s t)) θ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.scalar_polar_endpoint_bounds {u : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ}
  (hu : ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤) u)
  (hmean : rotationalScalarAverage u = 0) (a b : ℝ) :
  ∃ C > 0,
    ∀ s ∈ Set.Icc 0 Real.pi,
      ∀ θ ∈ Set.Icc a b,
        |u (polarSpherePoint s θ)| ≤ C * Real.sin s ∧
          |deriv (fun t ↦ u (polarSpherePoint t θ)) s| ≤ C ∧
            |deriv (fun t ↦ u (polarSpherePoint s t)) θ| ≤ C * Real.sin s
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.scalar_polar_boundary_limits {u : ↑RotationalSphere → ℝ}
  (hu : ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤) u)
  (hmean : rotationalScalarAverage u = 0) (θ : ℝ) :
  Filter.Tendsto (fun s ↦ Real.cos s * u (polarSpherePoint s θ) ^ 2) (nhds 0) (nhds 0) ∧
    Filter.Tendsto (fun s ↦ Real.cos s * u (polarSpherePoint s θ) ^ 2) (nhds Real.pi) (nhds 0)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.metric_inner_polarRadialVelocity
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (s theta : ℝ) :
  ((D.metric.inner (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint s theta))
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarRadialVelocity s theta))
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarRadialVelocity s theta) =
    D.a (Real.cos s) ^ 2 / D.b (Real.cos s)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.metric_inner_polarAngularVelocity
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (s theta : ℝ) :
  ((D.metric.inner (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint s theta))
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarAngularVelocity s theta))
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarAngularVelocity s theta) =
    D.b (Real.cos s) * Real.sin s ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.metric_inner_polarRadialAngular
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (s theta : ℝ) :
  ((D.metric.inner (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint s theta))
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarRadialVelocity s theta))
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarAngularVelocity s theta) =
    0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.normSq0S_polar_covector
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (s θ : ℝ) (hs : Real.sin s ≠ 0)
  (α :
    DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SSpace 1 (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint s θ)) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint s θ) 1 α =
    D.b (Real.cos s) / D.a (Real.cos s) ^ 2 * (α fun x ↦ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarRadialVelocity s θ) ^ 2 +
      1 / (D.b (Real.cos s) * Real.sin s ^ 2) * (α fun x ↦ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarAngularVelocity s θ) ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.normSq0S_differential1FormFun_polar
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  {u : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere → ℝ}
  (hu : ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤) u) (s θ : ℝ)
  (hs : Real.sin s ≠ 0) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint s θ) 1
      (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.differential1FormFun u
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint s θ)) =
    D.b (Real.cos s) / D.a (Real.cos s) ^ 2 *
        deriv (fun t ↦ u (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint t θ)) s ^ 2 +
      1 / (D.b (Real.cos s) * Real.sin s ^ 2) *
        deriv (fun t ↦ u (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint s t)) θ ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.integral_polar_metric
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  {F : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere → ℝ} (hF : Continuous F) :
  ∫ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
      F
        x ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
        (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
        D.metric =
    ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..Real.pi,
      ∫ (θ : ℝ) in 0..2 * Real.pi,
        D.a (Real.cos s) * Real.sin s * F (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.polarSpherePoint s θ)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.integral_scalar_energy_ge_of_rotationalScalarAverage_eq_zero
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  {u : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere → ℝ}
  (hu : ContMDiff (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ ℝ) (↑⊤) u)
  (hmean : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalScalarAverage u = 0) :
  ∫ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
      u x ^ 2 /
        D.b
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight
            x) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
        (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere)
        D.metric ≤
    ∫ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
      DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric x 1
          (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Operator.differential1FormFun u x) -
        1 / D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
          u x ^
            2 ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
        (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) D.metric
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.integral_weight_mul_normSq_eq_zero_iff.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  [CompactSpace M] {s : ℕ} (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s) (w : M → ℝ) (hw : Continuous w)
  (hpos : ∀ (x : M), 0 < w x) :
  ∫ (x : M),
        w x *
          DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x s
            (T x) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure I M g =
      0 ↔
    T = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.integral_weight_mul_sq_eq_zero_iff.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (u : M → ℝ) (hu : Continuous u) (w : M → ℝ) (hw : Continuous w)
  (hpos : ∀ (x : M), 0 < w x) :
  ∫ (x : M), w x * u x ^ 2 ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure I M g = 0 ↔ u = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.metricNabla0S_zero.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] {s : ℕ}
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) : DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g 0 = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.metricNabla0S_second_eq_zero_of_first_eq_zero.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  {s : ℕ} (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s)
  (hParallel : DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g T = 0) :
  DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g T) = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.roughLap0SField_eq_zero_of_metricNabla0S_eq_zero.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  {s : ℕ} (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (T : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.Tensor0SField (↑⊤) s)
  (hParallel : DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g T = 0) :
  DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.roughLap0SField g T = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.metricNabla0S_eq_zero_of_ahlfors_trace_curl_eq_zero.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  (hdim : Module.finrank ℝ E = 2) (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M)
  (Ω : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.TwoTensorSection)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection)
  (hAlt :
    ∀ (x : M) (v w : TangentSpace I x),
      (Ω x) (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 v w) =
        -(Ω x) (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.vec2 w v))
  (hUnit : ∀ (x : M), DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2 (Ω x) = 2)
  (hA : ahlforsPart g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h) = 0) (hTrace : oneFormTrace g h = 0)
  (hCurl : oneFormCurl g Ω h = 0) : DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation_eq_curvature_energy_of_metricNabla0S_eq_zero.{u_1} {M : Type u_1}
  [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M]
  [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection)
  (hParallel : DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h = 0) :
  oneFormDissipation g h =
    ∫ (x : M),
      (DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x / 2) ^ 2 *
        DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1
          (h
            x) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
        (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M g
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_remainder_lower_bound
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  have k := h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h;
  have μ :=
    DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
      (↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) D.metric;
  3 *
        ∫ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
          1 / D.a (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
            DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric x 2
              ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart D.metric
                  (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S D.metric k))
                x) ∂μ +
      1 / 2 *
        (∫ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormTrace D.metric k x ^ 2 /
              D.b (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) ∂μ +
          ∫ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormCurl D.metric D.areaForm k x ^ 2 /
              D.b (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) ∂μ) ≤
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric k
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_remainder_nonneg
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  0 ≤
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric
      (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_remainder_eq_zero_iff
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric
        (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h) =
      0 ↔
    h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_remainder_pos_iff
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  0 <
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric
        (h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h) ↔
    h - RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h ≠ 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_rotationalAverage_le
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h) ≤
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric h
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_eq_rotationalAverage_iff
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric h =
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h) ↔
    h = RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_eq_rotationalAverage_iff_invariant
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric h =
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric
        (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h) ↔
    ∀ (z : Circle),
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.diffeomorphTensorPullback (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.circleSphereDiffeo z)
          h =
        h
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.exists_haar_zonal_lower_bound
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  ∃ r s,
    ∃ (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hs : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) s),
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalAverage h = D.meridionalOneForm r hr + D.azimuthalOneForm s hs ∧
        2 * Real.pi * (D.meridionalAction r + D.meridionalAction s) ≤
            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric h ∧
          (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric h =
              2 * Real.pi * (D.meridionalAction r + D.meridionalAction s) ↔
            h = D.meridionalOneForm r hr + D.azimuthalOneForm s hs)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.continuousOn_log_reciprocal (a : ℝ → ℝ) (M : ℝ) (hM : 0 < M) (s : Set ℝ)
  (ha : ContinuousOn a s) (hpos : ∀ v ∈ s, 0 < a v) : ContinuousOn (fun v ↦ Real.log (M / a v)) s
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.hasDerivAt_log_reciprocal (a : ℝ → ℝ) (M v a' : ℝ) (hM : 0 < M) (ha : HasDerivAt a a' v)
  (hpos : 0 < a v) : HasDerivAt (fun x ↦ Real.log (M / a x)) (-a' / a v) v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.log_reciprocal_bounds (m M x : ℝ) (hm : 0 < m) (hl : m ≤ x) (hu : x ≤ M) :
  0 ≤ Real.log (M / x) ∧ Real.log (M / x) ≤ Real.log (M / m)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_eq_forward_intervalIntegral (k : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) :
  pairFunctional k = ∫ (v : ℝ) in 0..1, Real.exp (k v) * ∫ (s : ℝ) in v..1, 2 * s / Real.exp (k s)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_log_reciprocal (a : ℝ → ℝ) (M : ℝ) (hM : 0 < M)
  (ha : ContinuousOn a (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 < a v) :
  (pairFunctional fun v ↦ Real.log (M / a v)) = ∫ (v : ℝ) in 0..1, (2 * ∫ (s : ℝ) in v..1, s * a s) / a v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap : ℝ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.one_lt_criticalCap : 1 < criticalCap
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter_criticalCap : capParameter criticalCap = ((5 + √13) / 3) ^ (1 / 3)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.capParameter_criticalCap_cube : capParameter criticalCap ^ 3 = (5 + √13) / 3
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.upperContact_capParameter_criticalCap :
  upperContact (capParameter criticalCap) = (1 + √13) / 6
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_obstacleLogProfile_criticalCap :
  pairFunctional (obstacleLogProfile criticalCap) = 1 / 3
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_obstacleLogProfile_ge_one_third_iff (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C) :
  1 / 3 ≤ pairFunctional (obstacleLogProfile C) ↔ C ≤ criticalCap
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional_gt_one_third_of_differentiableOn (C : ℝ) (hC : 1 ≤ C)
  (hcap : C ≤ criticalCap) (k : ℝ → ℝ) (hk : ContinuousOn k (Set.Icc 0 1)) (hd : DifferentiableOn ℝ k (Set.Ioo 0 1))
  (hbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, 0 ≤ k v ∧ k v ≤ Real.log C) : 1 / 3 < pairFunctional k

  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  Prop
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.isConformalKillingOneForm_iff.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1} [NormedAddCommGroup E]
  [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H] {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H}
  {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  IsConformalKillingOneForm g h ↔
    ∀ (x : M), (ahlforsPart g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h)) x = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.isConformalKillingOneForm_iff_normSq_eq_zero.{u_1, u_2, u_3} {E : Type u_1}
  [NormedAddCommGroup E] [NormedSpace ℝ E] [FiniteDimensional ℝ E] {H : Type u_2} [TopologicalSpace H]
  {I : ModelWithCorners ℝ E H} {M : Type u_3} [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace H M] [IsManifold I (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric I M) (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  IsConformalKillingOneForm g h ↔
    ∀ (x : M),
      DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2
          ((ahlforsPart g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h)) x) =
        0

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.integral_meridionalWeight_eq_pairFunctional_sum
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (M : ℝ) (hM : 0 < M) :
  ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, D.meridionalWeight v =
    (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional fun v ↦ Real.log (M / D.a v)) +
      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional fun v ↦ Real.log (M / D.a (-v))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalDensity_const
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (c v : ℝ) :
  D.meridionalDensity (fun x ↦ c) v = (2 * D.meridionalWeight v - 2 * v ^ 2) * c ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.meridionalAction_const
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (c : ℝ) :
  (D.meridionalAction fun x ↦ c) = ((2 * ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, D.meridionalWeight v) - 4 / 3) * c ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_constant_zonal
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (c d : ℝ) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric
      (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯ + D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯) =
    2 * Real.pi * ((2 * ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, D.meridionalWeight v) - 4 / 3) * (c ^ 2 + d ^ 2)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.oneFormDissipation_constant_zonal_eq_pair_sum
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (M : ℝ) (hM : 0 < M) (c d : ℝ) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation D.metric
      (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯ + D.azimuthalOneForm (fun x ↦ d) ⋯) =
    2 * Real.pi *
        (2 *
            ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional fun v ↦ Real.log (M / D.a v)) +
              RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional fun v ↦ Real.log (M / D.a (-v))) -
          4 / 3) *
      (c ^ 2 + d ^ 2)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.isConformalKillingOneForm_meridional_const
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (c : ℝ) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.IsConformalKillingOneForm D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm (fun x ↦ c) ⋯)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.azimuthalOneForm_ahlfors_normSq
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (r : ℝ → ℝ) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r)
  (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere) :
  DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S D.metric x 2
      ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ahlforsPart D.metric
          (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S D.metric (D.azimuthalOneForm r hr)))
        x) =
    (D.meridionalWeight (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x) *
          deriv r (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.sphereHeight x)) ^
        2 /
      2

  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (c : ℝ) :

RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.curvature_bounds_iff_profile_bounds
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (κ Λ : ℝ) (hκ : 0 < κ) (hΛ : 0 < Λ) :
  (∀ (x : ↑RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalSphere),
      κ ≤ DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt D.metric x / 2 ∧
        DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt D.metric x / 2 ≤ Λ) ↔
    ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 1 / Λ ≤ D.a v ∧ D.a v ≤ 1 / κ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData.constant_zonal_coefficient_pos
  (D : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.PoleData) (m M : ℝ) (hm : 0 < m)
  (hb : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, m ≤ D.a v ∧ D.a v ≤ M) (hcap : M / m ≤ RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap) :
  0 <
    2 *
        ((RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional fun v ↦ Real.log (M / D.a v)) +
          RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.pairFunctional fun v ↦ Real.log (M / D.a (-v))) -
      4 / 3
