RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.integral_curvature (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, curvature a x = 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.warp_coordinate (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) (hbalance : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.balance a = 0)
  (v : ℝ) (hv : v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp (curvature a) (coordinate a v) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp a v / length a
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.integral_mul_curvature (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) (hbalance : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.balance a = 0) :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * curvature a x = 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.curvature_mem_Icc (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a) (m M : ℝ) (hm : 0 < m)
  (hbox : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, a v ∈ Set.Icc m M) (x : ℝ) : curvature a x ∈ Set.Icc (length a / M) (length a / m)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.meridionalAction_one_eq_integral (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) (hbalance : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.balance a = 0) :
  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction (curvature a) fun x ↦ 1) =
    ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, 2 * (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp a v / a v) - 2 * v ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.meridionalAction_one (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) (hbalance : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.balance a = 0) :
  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction (curvature a) fun x ↦ 1) =
    (2 * ∫ (v : ℝ) in -1..1, RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp a v / a v) - 4 / 3
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.coordinate_reflection (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) (heven : Function.Even a) (v : ℝ) : coordinate a (-v) = 1 - coordinate a v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.coordinate_zero (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) (heven : Function.Even a) : coordinate a 0 = 1 / 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.inverse_half (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) (heven : Function.Even a) : inverse a (1 / 2) = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.inverse_reflection (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) (heven : Function.Even a) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  inverse a (1 - x) = -inverse a x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.curvature_reflection (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) (heven : Function.Even a) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  curvature a (1 - x) = curvature a x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.curvature_antitoneOn_left (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) (heven : Function.Even a) (hmono : MonotoneOn a (Set.Icc (-1) 0)) :
  AntitoneOn (curvature a) (Set.Icc 0 (1 / 2))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.curvature_monotoneOn_right (a : ℝ → ℝ) (ha : Continuous a)
  (hpos : ∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1, 0 < a v) (heven : Function.Even a) (hanti : AntitoneOn a (Set.Icc 0 1)) :
  MonotoneOn (curvature a) (Set.Icc (1 / 2) 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.exists_contDiff_nonneg_tsupport_subset_integral_mul_pos (f : ℝ → ℝ) (a b l u : ℝ)
  (hf : ContinuousOn f (Set.Icc a b)) (hal : a ≤ l) (hlu : l < u) (hub : u ≤ b) (hfpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo l u, 0 < f x) :
  ∃ χ,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) χ ∧
      HasCompactSupport χ ∧ tsupport χ ⊆ Set.Ioo l u ∧ (∀ (x : ℝ), χ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) ∧ 0 < ∫ (x : ℝ) in a..b, f x * χ x
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.constantProbeVariationDensity : ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun f f1 K χ χ1 χ2 ↦ -2 * K * f ^ 2 * χ2 - 5 * K * f * f1 * χ1 + (4 * K ^ 2 * f - K * f1 ^ 2) * χ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalActionDensity_one_sub_mul (f f1 K χ χ1 χ2 ε : ℝ) :
  meridionalActionDensity f f1 K (1 - ε * χ) (-ε * χ1) (-ε * χ2) =
    meridionalActionDensity f f1 K 1 0 0 - ε * constantProbeVariationDensity f f1 K χ χ1 χ2 +
      ε ^ 2 * meridionalActionDensity f f1 K χ χ1 χ2
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.plateauFlux : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun K κ χ x ↦ -2 * κ * warp K x ^ 2 * deriv χ x - κ * warp K x * warpSlope K x * χ x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.plateauFlux_hasDerivAt (K χ : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hχ : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) χ) (κ l u : ℝ) (hSupp : tsupport χ ⊆ Set.Ioo l u) (hPlateau : ∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo l u, K x = κ)
  (x : ℝ) :
  HasDerivAt (plateauFlux K κ χ)
    (constantProbeVariationDensity (warp K x) (warpSlope K x) (K x) (χ x) (deriv χ x) (deriv (deriv χ) x) -
      2 * κ ^ 2 * warp K x * χ x)
    x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.plateauFlux_endpoints (K χ : ℝ → ℝ) (κ l u : ℝ) (hl : 0 < l) (hu : u < 1)
  (hSupp : tsupport χ ⊆ Set.Ioo l u) : plateauFlux K κ χ 0 = 0 ∧ plateauFlux K κ χ 1 = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.constantProbeVariation_integral_eq_plateau_pairing (K χ : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : Continuous K) (hχ : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) χ) (κ l u : ℝ) (hl : 0 < l) (hu : u < 1) (hSupp : tsupport χ ⊆ Set.Ioo l u)
  (hPlateau : ∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo l u, K x = κ) :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1,
      constantProbeVariationDensity (warp K x) (warpSlope K x) (K x) (χ x) (deriv χ x) (deriv (deriv χ) x) =
    2 * κ ^ 2 * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * χ x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction_one_sub_mul_of_plateau (K χ : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hχ : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) χ) (κ l u ε : ℝ) (hl : 0 < l) (hu : u < 1) (hSupp : tsupport χ ⊆ Set.Ioo l u)
  (hPlateau : ∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo l u, K x = κ) :
  ((deriv fun x ↦ 1 - ε * χ x) = fun x ↦ -ε * deriv χ x) ∧
    (deriv (deriv fun x ↦ 1 - ε * χ x) = fun x ↦ -ε * deriv (deriv χ) x) ∧
      (meridionalAction K fun x ↦ 1 - ε * χ x) =
        (meridionalAction K fun x ↦ 1) - 2 * ε * (κ ^ 2 * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * χ x) +
          ε ^ 2 * meridionalAction K χ
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_smooth_plateau_probe (K : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K) (κ l u : ℝ)
  (hκ : 0 < κ) (hl : 0 < l) (hlu : l < u) (hu : u < 1) (hPlateau : ∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo l u, K x = κ)
  (hfpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo l u, 0 < warp K x) (hZero : (meridionalAction K fun x ↦ 1) = 0) :
  ∃ χ,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) χ ∧
      HasCompactSupport χ ∧
        tsupport χ ⊆ Set.Ioo l u ∧
          (∀ (x : ℝ), χ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
            0 < ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * χ x ∧
              have A := κ ^ 2 * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, warp K x * χ x;
              have B := meridionalAction K χ;
              have ε := A / (|B| + 1);
              have r := fun x ↦ 1 - ε * χ x;
              0 < A ∧
                0 < ε ∧
                  ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r ∧
                    (deriv r = fun x ↦ -ε * deriv χ x) ∧
                      (deriv (deriv r) = fun x ↦ -ε * deriv (deriv χ) x) ∧
                        r 0 = 1 ∧
                          deriv r 0 = 0 ∧
                            (r =ᶠ[nhds 0] fun x ↦ 1) ∧
                              (r =ᶠ[nhds 1] fun x ↦ 1) ∧
                                (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ≠ 1) ∧
                                  meridionalAction K r = -2 * ε * A + ε ^ 2 * B ∧ meridionalAction K r < 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_smooth_probe_action_neg_of_constant_curvature_plateau (K : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : Continuous K) (κ l u : ℝ) (hκ : 0 < κ) (hl : 0 < l) (hlu : l < u) (hu : u < 1)
  (hPlateau : ∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo l u, K x = κ) (hfpos : ∀ x ∈ Set.Ioo l u, 0 < warp K x)
  (hZero : (meridionalAction K fun x ↦ 1) = 0) :
  ∃ r,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r ∧
      r 0 = 1 ∧
        deriv r 0 = 0 ∧
          (r =ᶠ[nhds 0] fun x ↦ 1) ∧ (r =ᶠ[nhds 1] fun x ↦ 1) ∧ (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ≠ 1) ∧ meridionalAction K r < 0
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.reciprocal : ℝ → ℝ :=
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.evenReciprocalProfile RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.length : ℝ :=
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.length reciprocal
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.coordinate : ℝ → ℝ :=
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.coordinate reciprocal
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.inverse : ℝ → ℝ :=
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.inverse reciprocal
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.curvature : ℝ → ℝ :=
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.ReciprocalArea.curvature reciprocal
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.length_pos : 0 < length
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.strict_average_bounds :
  length < 2 ∧ 2 < length * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.coordinate_hasDerivAt (v : ℝ) :
  HasDerivAt coordinate (reciprocal v / length) v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.coordinate_neg_one : coordinate (-1) = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.coordinate_one : coordinate 1 = 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.inverse_zero : inverse 0 = -1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.inverse_one : inverse 1 = 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.coordinate_bijOn :
  Set.BijOn coordinate (Set.Icc (-1) 1) (Set.Icc 0 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.inverse_mem (x : ℝ) : inverse x ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.inverse_coordinate (v : ℝ) (hv : v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1) :
  inverse (coordinate v) = v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.coordinate_inverse (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  coordinate (inverse x) = x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.inverse_continuous : Continuous inverse
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.curvature_continuous : Continuous curvature
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.curvature_pos (x : ℝ) : 0 < curvature x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.curvature_coordinate (v : ℝ) (hv : v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1) :
  curvature (coordinate v) = length / reciprocal v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.curvature_bounds (x : ℝ) :
  curvature x ∈ Set.Icc length (length * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.curvature_moments :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, curvature x = 2 ∧ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * curvature x = 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.warp_coordinate (v : ℝ) (hv : v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp curvature (coordinate v) =
    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.RotationalProfile.warp reciprocal v / length
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.warpSlope_coordinate (v : ℝ) (hv : v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warpSlope curvature (coordinate v) = -2 * v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.heightCoordinate_eq_inverse (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.heightCoordinate curvature x = inverse x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.coordinate_reflection (v : ℝ) : coordinate (-v) = 1 - coordinate v
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.coordinate_zero : coordinate 0 = 1 / 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.inverse_half : inverse 2⁻¹ = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.curvature_reflection (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  curvature (1 - x) = curvature x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.curvature_antitoneOn_left : AntitoneOn curvature (Set.Icc 0 (1 / 2))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.curvature_monotoneOn_right :
  MonotoneOn curvature (Set.Icc (1 / 2) 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.warp_pos (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1) :
  0 < RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp curvature x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.exists_positive_constant_plateau :
  ∃ l u,
    0 < l ∧
      l < u ∧
        u < 1 ∧
          (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc l u, curvature x = length) ∧
            ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc l u, 0 < RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp curvature x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.meridionalAction_one_eq_zero :
  (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction curvature fun x ↦ 1) = 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.exists_smooth_negative_probe_data :
  ∃ χ,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) χ ∧
      HasCompactSupport χ ∧
        tsupport χ ⊆ Set.Ioo 0 1 ∧
          (∀ x ∈ tsupport χ, curvature x = length) ∧
            (∀ (x : ℝ), χ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
              0 < ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp curvature x * χ x ∧
                have A :=
                  length ^ 2 * ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp curvature x * χ x;
                have B := RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction curvature χ;
                have ε := A / (|B| + 1);
                have r := fun x ↦ 1 - ε * χ x;
                0 < A ∧
                  0 < ε ∧
                    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r ∧
                      (deriv r = fun x ↦ -ε * deriv χ x) ∧
                        (deriv (deriv r) = fun x ↦ -ε * deriv (deriv χ) x) ∧
                          r 0 = 1 ∧
                            deriv r 0 = 0 ∧
                              (r =ᶠ[nhds 0] fun x ↦ 1) ∧
                                (r =ᶠ[nhds 1] fun x ↦ 1) ∧
                                  (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ≠ 1) ∧
                                    RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction curvature r =
                                        -2 * ε * A + ε ^ 2 * B ∧
                                      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction curvature r < 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.exists_smooth_negative_probe :
  ∃ r,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r ∧
      r 0 = 1 ∧
        deriv r 0 = 0 ∧
          (r =ᶠ[nhds 0] fun x ↦ 1) ∧
            (r =ᶠ[nhds 1] fun x ↦ 1) ∧
              (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ≠ 1) ∧
                RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction curvature r < 0
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.bernsteinPolynomialOfCoefficients : (n : ℕ) → (Fin (n + 1) → ℝ) → Polynomial ℝ :=
fun n coeff ↦ ∑ j, Polynomial.C (coeff j) * bernsteinPolynomial ℝ n ↑j
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.eval_bernsteinPolynomialOfCoefficients (n : ℕ) (coeff : Fin (n + 1) → ℝ) (x : ℝ) :
  Polynomial.eval x (bernsteinPolynomialOfCoefficients n coeff) =
    ∑ j, ↑(n.choose ↑j) * x ^ ↑j * (1 - x) ^ (n - ↑j) * coeff j
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.eval_bernsteinPolynomialOfCoefficients_unitInterval (n : ℕ) (coeff : Fin (n + 1) → ℝ)
  (x : ↑unitInterval) :
  Polynomial.eval (↑x) (bernsteinPolynomialOfCoefficients n coeff) = ∑ j, (bernstein n ↑j) x * coeff j
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.derivative_bernsteinPolynomialOfCoefficients_succ (n : ℕ) (coeff : Fin (n + 2) → ℝ) :
  Polynomial.derivative (bernsteinPolynomialOfCoefficients (n + 1) coeff) =
    Polynomial.C (↑n + 1) * bernsteinPolynomialOfCoefficients n fun k ↦ coeff k.succ - coeff k.castSucc
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.sampledBernsteinPolynomial : ℕ → (ℝ → ℝ) → Polynomial ℝ :=
fun n f ↦ bernsteinPolynomialOfCoefficients n fun j ↦ f (↑↑j / ↑n)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.eval_sampledBernsteinPolynomial (n : ℕ) (f : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ↑unitInterval) :
  Polynomial.eval (↑x) (sampledBernsteinPolynomial n f) = ∑ j, (bernstein n ↑j) x * f ↑(bernstein.z j)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.bernsteinPolynomialOfCoefficients_mem_Icc (n : ℕ) (coeff : Fin (n + 1) → ℝ) {lo hi : ℝ}
  (hcoeff : ∀ (j : Fin (n + 1)), coeff j ∈ Set.Icc lo hi) {x : ℝ} (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  Polynomial.eval x (bernsteinPolynomialOfCoefficients n coeff) ∈ Set.Icc lo hi
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.monotoneOn_sampledBernsteinPolynomial (f : ℝ → ℝ) (hf : MonotoneOn f (Set.Icc 0 1))
  (n : ℕ) : MonotoneOn (fun x ↦ Polynomial.eval x (sampledBernsteinPolynomial (n + 1) f)) (Set.Icc 0 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.tendsto_sampledBernsteinPolynomial (f : ℝ → ℝ) {M : ℝ}
  (hbound : ∀ y ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, |f y| ≤ M) {x : ℝ} (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1)
  (hcont : ContinuousWithinAt f (Set.Icc 0 1) x) :
  Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ Polynomial.eval x (sampledBernsteinPolynomial (n + 1) f)) Filter.atTop (nhds (f x))
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.squaredMidpointRadius : ℝ → ℝ :=
fun x ↦ (2 * x - 1) ^ 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.squaredMidpointRadius_mem_Icc {x : ℝ} (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  squaredMidpointRadius x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.upperRadialProfile : (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun K t ↦ K ((1 + √t) / 2)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.upperRadialArgument_mem_Icc {t : ℝ} (ht : t ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  (1 + √t) / 2 ∈ Set.Icc (1 / 2) 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.monotoneOn_upperRadialProfile (K : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : MonotoneOn K (Set.Icc (1 / 2) 1)) :
  MonotoneOn (upperRadialProfile K) (Set.Icc 0 1)
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.symmetricBernsteinApproximation : ℕ → (ℝ → ℝ) → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun n K x ↦ Polynomial.eval (squaredMidpointRadius x) (sampledBernsteinPolynomial (n + 1) (upperRadialProfile K))
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.symmetricBernsteinPolynomial : ℕ → (ℝ → ℝ) → Polynomial ℝ :=
fun n K ↦ (sampledBernsteinPolynomial (n + 1) (upperRadialProfile K)).comp ((Polynomial.C 2 * Polynomial.X - 1) ^ 2)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.eval_symmetricBernsteinPolynomial (n : ℕ) (K : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) :
  Polynomial.eval x (symmetricBernsteinPolynomial n K) = symmetricBernsteinApproximation n K x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.contDiff_symmetricBernsteinApproximation (n : ℕ) (K : ℝ → ℝ) :
  ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (symmetricBernsteinApproximation n K)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.symmetric_symmetricBernsteinApproximation (n : ℕ) (K : ℝ → ℝ) (x : ℝ) :
  symmetricBernsteinApproximation n K (1 - x) = symmetricBernsteinApproximation n K x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.monotoneOn_symmetricBernsteinApproximation (n : ℕ) (K : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : MonotoneOn K (Set.Icc (1 / 2) 1)) : MonotoneOn (symmetricBernsteinApproximation n K) (Set.Icc (1 / 2) 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.antitoneOn_symmetricBernsteinApproximation (n : ℕ) (K : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : MonotoneOn K (Set.Icc (1 / 2) 1)) : AntitoneOn (symmetricBernsteinApproximation n K) (Set.Icc 0 (1 / 2))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.symmetricBernsteinApproximation_mem_Icc (n : ℕ) (K : ℝ → ℝ) (lo hi : ℝ)
  (hbox : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, K x ∈ Set.Icc lo hi) {x : ℝ} (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  symmetricBernsteinApproximation n K x ∈ Set.Icc lo hi
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.upperRadialProfile_squaredMidpointRadius_eq (K : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hsymm : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, K (1 - x) = K x) {x : ℝ} (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  upperRadialProfile K (squaredMidpointRadius x) = K x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.L1Subsequence.exists_strictMono_ae_tendsto_of_integral_norm_tendsto_zero.{u_1}
  {X : Type u_1} [MeasurableSpace X] (mu : MeasureTheory.Measure X) (f : ℕ → X → ℝ) (g : X → ℝ)
  (hIntegrable : ∀ (n : ℕ), MeasureTheory.Integrable (fun x ↦ f n x - g x) mu)
  (hL1 : Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : X), ‖f n x - g x‖ ∂mu) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) :
  ∃ ns, StrictMono ns ∧ ∀ᵐ (x : X) ∂mu, Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ f (ns n) x) Filter.atTop (nhds (g x))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.SymmetricMomentRecovery.firstMoment_eq_half_integral_of_reflection (f : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hf : IntervalIntegrable f MeasureTheory.volume 0 1) (hsymm : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, f (1 - x) = f x) :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * f x = (∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, f x) / 2
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Analysis.SymmetricMomentRecovery.exists_smooth_exactMoments_mem_box_l1_ae (m lo hi : ℝ)
  (hLower : lo < m) (hUpper : m < hi) (K : ℝ → ℝ) (hMeasurable : MeasureTheory.StronglyMeasurable fun x ↦ K ↑x)
  (hSymm : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, K (1 - x) = K x) (hMono : MonotoneOn K (Set.Icc (1 / 2) 1))
  (hMoment : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = m) (hBox : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, K x ∈ Set.Icc lo hi) :
  ∃ G,
    (∀ (n : ℕ),
        ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) (G n) ∧
          (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, G n (1 - x) = G n x) ∧
            MonotoneOn (G n) (Set.Icc (1 / 2) 1) ∧
              AntitoneOn (G n) (Set.Icc 0 (1 / 2)) ∧
                (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, G n x ∈ Set.Icc lo hi) ∧
                  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, G n x = m ∧ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * G n x = m / 2) ∧
      Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, |G n x - K x|) Filter.atTop (nhds 0) ∧
        ∃ ns,
          StrictMono ns ∧
            ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Icc 0 1),
              Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ G (ns n) x) Filter.atTop (nhds (K x))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.abs_warp_sub_le_integral_abs (K L : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hL : Continuous L) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) : |warp K x - warp L x| ≤ 2 * ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, |K s - L s|
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.abs_warpSlope_sub_le_integral_abs (K L : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hL : Continuous L) (x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  |warpSlope K x - warpSlope L x| ≤ 2 * ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..1, |K s - L s|
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.tendsto_warp_of_l1 (Kn : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ) (K : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hn : ∀ (n : ℕ), Continuous (Kn n)) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hL1 : Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, |Kn n x - K x|) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) (x : ℝ)
  (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) : Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ warp (Kn n) x) Filter.atTop (nhds (warp K x))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.tendsto_warpSlope_of_l1 (Kn : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ) (K : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hn : ∀ (n : ℕ), Continuous (Kn n)) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hL1 : Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, |Kn n x - K x|) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) (x : ℝ)
  (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) : Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ warpSlope (Kn n) x) Filter.atTop (nhds (warpSlope K x))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.tendstoUniformlyOn_warp_of_l1 (Kn : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ) (K : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hn : ∀ (n : ℕ), Continuous (Kn n)) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hL1 : Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, |Kn n x - K x|) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) :
  TendstoUniformlyOn (fun n ↦ warp (Kn n)) (warp K) Filter.atTop (Set.Icc 0 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.tendstoUniformlyOn_warpSlope_of_l1 (Kn : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ) (K : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hn : ∀ (n : ℕ), Continuous (Kn n)) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hL1 : Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, |Kn n x - K x|) Filter.atTop (nhds 0)) :
  TendstoUniformlyOn (fun n ↦ warpSlope (Kn n)) (warpSlope K) Filter.atTop (Set.Icc 0 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.tendsto_meridionalAction_of_l1_of_ae (Kn : ℕ → ℝ → ℝ) (K r : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hn : ∀ (n : ℕ), Continuous (Kn n)) (hK : Continuous K) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (B : ℝ)
  (hbound : ∀ (n : ℕ), ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, |Kn n x| ≤ B)
  (hL1 : Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, |Kn n x - K x|) Filter.atTop (nhds 0))
  (hae :
    ∀ᵐ (x : ℝ) ∂MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Icc 0 1),
      Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ Kn n x) Filter.atTop (nhds (K x))) :
  Filter.Tendsto (fun n ↦ meridionalAction (Kn n) r) Filter.atTop (nhds (meridionalAction K r))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warp_affine_two (K : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K) (t x : ℝ) :
  warp (fun s ↦ (1 - t) * K s + 2 * t) x = (1 - t) * warp K x + t * warp (fun x ↦ 2) x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.warpSlope_affine_two (K : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K) (t x : ℝ) :
  warpSlope (fun s ↦ (1 - t) * K s + 2 * t) x = (1 - t) * warpSlope K x + t * warpSlope (fun x ↦ 2) x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.continuous_meridionalAction_affine (K r : ℝ → ℝ) (hK : Continuous K)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) : Continuous fun t ↦ meridionalAction (fun x ↦ (1 - t) * K x + 2 * t) r
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation_squares_integrable.{u_1} {M : Type u_1} [TopologicalSpace M]
  [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M] [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M]
  [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M) (κ : ℝ)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  MeasureTheory.Integrable
    (fun x ↦
      DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1
            ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.roughLap0SField g h) x + κ • h x) +
          κ *
            DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2
              ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h) x) +
        2 * κ *
          DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2
            ((ahlforsPart g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h)) x))
    (DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M
      g)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation_eq_integral_squares_of_constant_curvature.{u_1} {M : Type u_1}
  [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M]
  [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M) (κ : ℝ)
  (hK : ∀ (x : M), DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x / 2 = κ)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) :
  oneFormDissipation g h =
    ∫ (x : M),
      DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 1
            ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.roughLap0SField g h) x + κ • h x) +
          κ *
            DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2
              ((DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h) x) +
        2 * κ *
          DifferentialGeometry.Tensor0SBundle.normSq0S g x 2
            ((ahlforsPart g (DifferentialGeometry.PDE.RicciFlow.metricNabla0S g h))
              x) ∂DifferentialGeometry.Integral.Measure.riemannianVolumeMeasure
        (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M g
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.oneFormDissipation_nonneg_of_constant_curvature.{u_1} {M : Type u_1}
  [TopologicalSpace M] [ChartedSpace (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)) M]
  [IsManifold (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) (↑⊤) M] [T2Space M] [CompactSpace M]
  (g : DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2))) M) (κ : ℝ)
  (hκ : 0 ≤ κ) (hK : ∀ (x : M), DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x / 2 = κ)
  (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection) : 0 ≤ oneFormDissipation g h
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.contractedCurvature : ℝ → ℝ → ℝ :=
fun t x ↦ (1 - t) * curvature x + 2 * t
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.contractedLower : ℝ → ℝ :=
fun t ↦ (1 - t) * length + 2 * t
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.contractedUpper : ℝ → ℝ :=
fun t ↦ (1 - t) * length * RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap + 2 * t
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.contractedCurvature_continuous (t : ℝ) :
  Continuous (contractedCurvature t)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.contractedCurvature_moments (t : ℝ) :
  ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, contractedCurvature t x = 2 ∧ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * contractedCurvature t x = 1
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.contractedCurvature_reflection (t x : ℝ) (hx : x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1) :
  contractedCurvature t (1 - x) = contractedCurvature t x
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.contractedCurvature_antitoneOn_left (t : ℝ) (ht : t ≤ 1) :
  AntitoneOn (contractedCurvature t) (Set.Icc 0 (1 / 2))
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.contractedCurvature_monotoneOn_right (t : ℝ) (ht : t ≤ 1) :
  MonotoneOn (contractedCurvature t) (Set.Icc (1 / 2) 1)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.contractedCurvature_bounds (t : ℝ) (ht : t ≤ 1) (x : ℝ) :
  contractedCurvature t x ∈ Set.Icc (contractedLower t) (contractedUpper t)
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.contracted_strict_bounds (t : ℝ) (ht0 : 0 < t) (ht1 : t < 1) :
  0 < contractedLower t ∧ contractedLower t < 2 ∧ 2 < contractedUpper t
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.contracted_ratio_deficit (t : ℝ) (hlo : contractedLower t ≠ 0) :
  RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap - contractedUpper t / contractedLower t =
    2 * t * (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap - 1) / contractedLower t
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.contracted_ratio_deficit_pos (t : ℝ) (ht0 : 0 < t) (ht1 : t < 1) :
  0 < 2 * t * (RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap - 1) / contractedLower t
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.contracted_ratio_lt_criticalCap (t : ℝ) (ht0 : 0 < t)
  (ht1 : t < 1) : contractedUpper t / contractedLower t < RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.exists_negative_contraction_parameter_of_negative_action (r : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (hneg : RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction curvature r < 0) :
  ∃ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction (contractedCurvature t) r < 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.exists_smooth_negative_contraction :
  ∃ r t,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r ∧
      r 0 = 1 ∧
        deriv r 0 = 0 ∧
          (r =ᶠ[nhds 0] fun x ↦ 1) ∧
            (r =ᶠ[nhds 1] fun x ↦ 1) ∧
              (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ≠ 1) ∧
                0 < t ∧
                  t < 1 ∧
                    0 < contractedLower t ∧
                      contractedLower t < 2 ∧
                        2 < contractedUpper t ∧
                          contractedUpper t / contractedLower t < RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap ∧
                            RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction (contractedCurvature t) r < 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.exists_smooth_negative_action_of_symmetric_box (K r : ℝ → ℝ)
  (hK : Continuous K) (hr : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r) (lo hi : ℝ) (hlo : lo < 2) (hhi : 2 < hi)
  (hSymm : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, K (1 - x) = K x) (hMono : MonotoneOn K (Set.Icc (1 / 2) 1))
  (hMoment : ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2) (hBox : ∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, K x ∈ Set.Icc lo hi)
  (hNeg : meridionalAction K r < 0) :
  ∃ G,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) G ∧
      (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, G (1 - x) = G x) ∧
        MonotoneOn G (Set.Icc (1 / 2) 1) ∧
          AntitoneOn G (Set.Icc 0 (1 / 2)) ∧
            (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, G x ∈ Set.Icc lo hi) ∧
              ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, G x = 2 ∧ ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * G x = 1 ∧ meridionalAction G r < 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.CriticalAreaProfile.exists_smooth_negative_area_data :
  ∃ r t K,
    ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r ∧
      r 0 = 1 ∧
        deriv r 0 = 0 ∧
          (r =ᶠ[nhds 0] fun x ↦ 1) ∧
            (r =ᶠ[nhds 1] fun x ↦ 1) ∧
              (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ≠ 1) ∧
                0 < t ∧
                  t < 1 ∧
                    0 < contractedLower t ∧
                      contractedUpper t / contractedLower t < RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Variational.criticalCap ∧
                        ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) K ∧
                          (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, K x ∈ Set.Icc (contractedLower t) (contractedUpper t)) ∧
                            ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2 ∧
                              ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1 ∧
                                (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, K (1 - x) = K x) ∧
                                  MonotoneOn K (Set.Icc (1 / 2) 1) ∧
                                    AntitoneOn K (Set.Icc 0 (1 / 2)) ∧
                                      RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.AreaProfile.meridionalAction K r < 0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.exists_smooth_negative_geometric_realization :
  ∃ r t K X D R,
    ∃ (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R),
      ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) r ∧
        r 0 = 1 ∧
          deriv r 0 = 0 ∧
            (r =ᶠ[nhds 0] fun x ↦ 1) ∧
              (r =ᶠ[nhds 1] fun x ↦ 1) ∧
                (∃ x ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1, r x ≠ 1) ∧
                  0 < t ∧
                    t < 1 ∧
                      0 < CriticalAreaProfile.contractedLower t ∧
                        CriticalAreaProfile.contractedUpper t / CriticalAreaProfile.contractedLower t <
                            conformalKillingThreshold ∧
                          ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) K ∧
                            (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
                                K x ∈
                                  Set.Icc (CriticalAreaProfile.contractedLower t)
                                    (CriticalAreaProfile.contractedUpper t)) ∧
                              ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, K x = 2 ∧
                                ∫ (x : ℝ) in 0..1, x * K x = 1 ∧
                                  (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1, K (1 - x) = K x) ∧
                                    MonotoneOn K (Set.Icc (1 / 2) 1) ∧
                                      AntitoneOn K (Set.Icc 0 (1 / 2)) ∧
                                        ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) X ∧
                                          AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K 0 = -1 ∧
                                            AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K 1 = 1 ∧
                                              X (-1) = 0 ∧
                                                X 1 = 1 ∧
                                                  Set.MapsTo (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K) (Set.Icc 0 1)
                                                      (Set.Icc (-1) 1) ∧
                                                    Set.MapsTo X (Set.Icc (-1) 1) (Set.Icc 0 1) ∧
                                                      (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
                                                          X (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K x) = x ∧
                                                            D.a (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K x) = 1 / K x ∧
                                                              R (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K x) = r x ∧
                                                                RotationalProfile.warp D.a
                                                                    (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K x) =
                                                                  AreaProfile.warp K x) ∧
                                                        (∀ v ∈ Set.Icc (-1) 1,
                                                            AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K (X v) = v ∧
                                                              deriv X v * K (X v) = 1) ∧
                                                          R = r ∘ X ∧
                                                            (∀ x ∈ Set.Icc 0 1,
                                                                deriv D.a (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K x) =
                                                                    -deriv K x / K x ^ 3 ∧
                                                                  deriv R (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K x) =
                                                                      deriv r x / K x ∧
                                                                    deriv (deriv R) (AreaProfile.heightCoordinate K x) =
                                                                      deriv (deriv r) x / K x ^ 2 -
                                                                        deriv r x * deriv K x / K x ^ 3) ∧
                                                              (∀ (p : ↑RotationalSphere),
                                                                  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt
                                                                        D.metric p /
                                                                      2 =
                                                                    K (X (sphereHeight p))) ∧
                                                                oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm R hR) =
                                                                    2 * Real.pi * AreaProfile.meridionalAction K r ∧
                                                                  IsRepresentedRotationalMetric D.metric ∧
                                                                    (∀ (p : ↑RotationalSphere),
                                                                        CriticalAreaProfile.contractedLower t ≤
                                                                            DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt
                                                                                D.metric p /
                                                                              2 ∧
                                                                          DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt
                                                                                D.metric p /
                                                                              2 ≤
                                                                            CriticalAreaProfile.contractedUpper t) ∧
                                                                      D.meridionalOneForm R hR ≠ 0 ∧
                                                                        oneFormDissipation D.metric
                                                                            (D.meridionalOneForm R hR) <
                                                                          0
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.exists_negative_rotational_below_conformalKillingThreshold :
  ∃ t D R,
    ∃ (hR : ContDiff ℝ (↑⊤) R),
      0 < t ∧
        t < 1 ∧
          0 < CriticalAreaProfile.contractedLower t ∧
            IsRepresentedRotationalMetric D.metric ∧
              (∀ (p : ↑RotationalSphere),
                  CriticalAreaProfile.contractedLower t ≤
                      DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt D.metric p / 2 ∧
                    DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt D.metric p / 2 ≤
                      CriticalAreaProfile.contractedUpper t) ∧
                D.meridionalOneForm R hR ≠ 0 ∧
                  oneFormDissipation D.metric (D.meridionalOneForm R hR) < 0 ∧
                    CriticalAreaProfile.contractedUpper t / CriticalAreaProfile.contractedLower t <
                      conformalKillingThreshold
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalSafeCaps : Set ℝ :=
{C |
  1 ≤ C ∧
    ∀
      (g :
        DifferentialGeometry.SmoothRiemannianMetric (modelWithCornersSelf ℝ (EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 2)))
          ↑RotationalSphere),
      IsRepresentedRotationalMetric g →
        ∀ (κ Λ : ℝ),
          0 < κ →
            (∀ (x : ↑RotationalSphere),
                κ ≤ DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x / 2 ∧
                  DifferentialGeometry.Geometry.Curvature.metricScalarAt g x / 2 ≤ Λ) →
              Λ / κ ≤ C → ∀ (h : DifferentialGeometry.Tensor.RicciIdentity.OneFormSection), 0 ≤ oneFormDissipation g h}
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.one_mem_rotationalSafeCaps : 1 ∈ rotationalSafeCaps
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalSafeCaps_nonempty : rotationalSafeCaps.Nonempty
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalSafeCaps_downward {C D : ℝ} (hC : C ∈ rotationalSafeCaps) (hD : 1 ≤ D)
  (hDC : D ≤ C) : D ∈ rotationalSafeCaps
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.exists_contracted_bound_for_rotationalSafeCaps :
  ∃ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1,
    CriticalAreaProfile.contractedUpper t / CriticalAreaProfile.contractedLower t < conformalKillingThreshold ∧
      ∀ C ∈ rotationalSafeCaps, C < CriticalAreaProfile.contractedUpper t / CriticalAreaProfile.contractedLower t
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalSafeCaps_bddAbove : BddAbove rotationalSafeCaps
def RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalThreshold : ℝ :=
sSup rotationalSafeCaps
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.one_le_rotationalThreshold : 1 ≤ rotationalThreshold
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.exists_contracted_ratio_above_rotationalThreshold :
  ∃ t ∈ Set.Ioo 0 1,
    rotationalThreshold ≤ CriticalAreaProfile.contractedUpper t / CriticalAreaProfile.contractedLower t ∧
      CriticalAreaProfile.contractedUpper t / CriticalAreaProfile.contractedLower t < conformalKillingThreshold
RicciFlowSharpEstimate.Geometry.rotationalThreshold_lt_conformalKillingThreshold :
  rotationalThreshold < conformalKillingThreshold
